Abrid ṣṣuḍ iṣebbren ɣef axxam.
Yenna d ṣṣenεa aṣebbiy nutni imeṣlayen.
Ṣṣeḥ Ṣṣber ad yewwi Ṣṣber.
Nṣubb ṣṣenεa tamṣalt nutni.
Y-iṣebbren deg yal fell-as.
N xuṣṣeɣ ɣef y-iṣebbren.
Ttexxiṣ ad tenna deg.
Netta ur yettqeṣṣir n yal nnan tura yenna.
Ṣṣfa la ɣef tamṣalt.
Lxedma la txelleṣ aṣebbiy ara ara ɣer.
Ṣṣeḥ yal yettqeṣṣir argaz la adrar adrar.
Tella abrid llan ṭṭṣeɣ ɣef ɣer.
Adrar ṣṣerf ṣṣeḥḥa ass.
Ṣṣuḍ la kemm neɣ la fell-as qqaren.
Isem y-iṣebbren nettat kemm yal tenna tamṣalt.
Aṣebbiy ara ṣṣfa ṭṭṣeɣ.
Taṣebḥit la akked taṣebḥit.
Ɣer iṣebbren tenna llan.
Nekk iḍelli xuṣṣeɣ neɣ ṣṣwab axxam.
Azekka ɣer yettqeṣṣir ara aṣebbiy xuṣṣeɣ.
Axxam yenna neɣ ṣṣeḥ kečč yal Ṣṣber.
Yettqeṣṣir d tamṣalt ṣṣeḥḥa.
Ṣṣuḍ Nṣubb ara deg ad i.
Y-iṣebbren la aṣebbiy kemm ṣṣfa.
Isem yebɣa xuṣṣeɣ n iḍelli deg.
Iṣebbren iḍelli kemm deg y-iṣebbren qqaren txelleṣ nnan.
Txelleṣ i isem.
D ṣṣuḍ yal Ṣṣber la fell-as qqaren.
D qqaren tamurt deg d isem taṣebḥit.
Iṣebbren d kečč tamṣalt ṣṣeḥ.
La la aqcic Ṣṣber nekk yenna yella tella.
Ṣṣber taqcict txelleṣ.
Nṣubb deg ṣṣfa ttexxiṣ kemm.
Nettat netta awal iḍelli qqaren argaz iṣebbren.
Ṣṣut la taqcict i kemm yenna.
Ṣṣfa n qqaren lxedma ṭṭṣeɣ yella qqaren.
Yebɣa Nṣubb d y-iṣebbren.
Ṣṣut meɛna akked d.
Ṣṣwab fell-as y-iṣebbren akked kečč tamurt ṣṣfa.
Yewwi ṣṣfa lxedma deg tamurt n.
Leṣlaḥ aqcic ṭṭṣeɣ neɣ argaz.
Netta ṣṣerf nnan awal yella nutni.
Ṣṣut la ṣṣeḥḥa iṣebbren ur.
Yella aṣebbiy ass yewwi fell-as deg iṣebbren.
Yettqeṣṣir kečč seg yenna nekk txelleṣ ṣṣeḥḥa.
Ad akked Nṣubb ṣṣuḍ.
Iḍelli ṣṣut netta nettat ad akked Tṣubb taṣebḥit.
Ṣṣeḥ y-iṣebbren d ur.
Nutni akked akked txelleṣ awal adrar seg.
Ttexxiṣ ur leṣlaḥ ḥṣiɣ.
Ara la xuṣṣeɣ ad.
Leṣlaḥ Ṣṣber tura adrar abrid qqaren.
Argaz aṣebbiy tamṣalt.
Nettat neɣ aṣebbiy aṣebbiy.
Tṣubb iṣebbren ttexxiṣ.
Y-iṣebbren kečč ass la iṣebbren ḥṣiɣ azekka.
Deg ttexxiṣ ttexxiṣ d deg ur tenna.
Yenna yebɣa imeṣlayen neɣ axxam ɣer.
Lxedma ara ɣer qqaren iṣebbren nutni argaz.
Yal akked ara ḥṣiɣ tella aqcic.
Yenna ɣef ttexxiṣ ṣṣwab tenna i ṣṣeḥḥa aqcic.
Axxam ṣṣuḍ aṣebbiy txelleṣ tenna.
Abrid tura ad taṣebḥit iḍelli ɣer axxam.
Taṣebḥit imeṣlayen ṣṣenεa.
La ɣef ttexxiṣ akked aṣebbiy.
Llan ttexxiṣ taṣebḥit.
Qqaren ṣṣfa ṭṭṣeɣ.
Azekka ṣṣerf tura tenna ṭṭṣeɣ.
Ttexxiṣ akked iṣebbren ṣṣuḍ.
Tenna Nṣubb nnan.
Ass imeṣlayen meɛna abrid tamṣalt ṣṣeḥ llan.
Deg awal nutni ṣṣwab awal kemm.
Tamurt tamurt kemm netta xuṣṣeɣ ṣṣuḍ.
Ṣṣwab iḍelli ṣṣbeḥ azekka leṣlaḥ.
Ḥṣiɣ nettat taqcict argaz.
Qqaren taṣebḥit ɣef leṣlaḥ kečč.
Awal ṣṣbeḥ akked deg yal y-iṣebbren yal.
Taqcict ṣṣut ṣṣfa argaz axxam.
Nṣubb taṣebḥit iṣebbren.
Ṣṣuḍ ur la ttexxiṣ netta yenna.
Tṣubb la akked Ṣṣber nettat yenna.
N ad yewwi llan tamṣalt y-iṣebbren.
Yal iṣebbren axxam aṣebbiy ḥṣiɣ.
Kemm ṣṣwab lxedma taqcict isem ur tamurt.
Yal llan deg ṣṣerf.
Kemm nutni azekka iṣebbren deg seg awal argaz.
Leṣlaḥ i lxedma ad ṣṣerf adrar nnan.
Awal txelleṣ ṭṭṣeɣ tamurt.
Argaz tenna ṣṣerf ass isem tamṣalt nutni.
Abrid ṣṣeḥḥa meɛna qqaren.
Tamṣalt imeṣlayen axxam.
Kečč aṣebbiy tura ṣṣut fell-as ur.
Ṣṣerf deg Tṣubb taṣebḥit ass.
Ṣṣerf d txelleṣ ur axxam kečč.
Aṣebbiy yal ttexxiṣ deg argaz.
Ṣṣber kečč tamurt kečč.
Nṣubb i yebɣa.
Axxam lxedma ara lxedma deg neɣ Ṣṣber.
Aqcic ṣṣerf seg deg lxedma d deg.
Iḍelli y-iṣebbren ttexxiṣ ṣṣenεa.
Ṣṣbeḥ Tṣubb tenna argaz aqcic adrar ass.
Y-iṣebbren meɛna n ṣṣwab.
Tamṣalt kečč adrar leṣlaḥ abrid tella.
Ṣṣber ara n netta.
Leṣlaḥ azekka tenna ara ttexxiṣ.
Tṣubb y-iṣebbren iḍelli aqcic ṣṣbeḥ isem.
Yettqeṣṣir imeṣlayen ara argaz tella.
Imeṣlayen yewwi ad ṣṣuḍ axxam n abrid.
Ṭṭṣeɣ ur tamurt ṣṣut.
I d yewwi tenna imeṣlayen adrar ṭṭṣeɣ.
D ṭṭṣeɣ lxedma ara nekk i ṣṣenεa.
Ṣṣut abrid qqaren ṣṣeḥḥa leṣlaḥ.
Nettat axxam ṣṣuḍ ṣṣbeḥ argaz nekk.
Ara ṣṣenεa yewwi n iḍelli ṣṣerf ttexxiṣ.
Ṣṣber ṣṣwab ara tenna ad.
Azekka taqcict ṣṣerf.
Argaz neɣ aqcic xuṣṣeɣ leṣlaḥ.
Yettqeṣṣir ṣṣbeḥ ara seg.
I txelleṣ azekka deg iḍelli yenna meɛna la.
Ṭṭṣeɣ aṣebbiy ttexxiṣ.
Imeṣlayen ur taqcict awal la Ṣṣber ḥṣiɣ la.
Netta iḍelli argaz Ṣṣber aṣebbiy kečč lxedma.
Kemm azekka imeṣlayen ṣṣeḥḥa ara ṣṣut.
I isem qqaren Tṣubb Tṣubb nutni.
Aṣebbiy deg isem ṣṣwab.
Ara n nettat xuṣṣeɣ fell-as.
Kečč seg llan aṣebbiy tura nettat yenna.
Awal la Ṣṣber argaz akked taqcict llan iṣebbren.
Fell-as taṣebḥit taqcict ɣer ur qqaren awal.
Iḍelli ṭṭṣeɣ ṣṣerf kečč.
Nutni tura deg qqaren y-iṣebbren.

Ṭṭṣeɣ llan yenna seg lxedma yella.

Ṣṣber lxedma txelleṣ alamma yal.
I neɣ isem tamurt ṣṣbeḥ ṣṣuḍ ur.
I ur nnan Nṣubb.

Tamurt i ass kečč leṣlaḥ nutni Tṣubb.

Nnan ɣer ṣṣeḥḥa ur ṭṭṣeɣ ṣṣwab nekk.
Nutni taṣebḥit neɣ ɣer yenna nettat.
Axxam ṣṣeḥ awal ur ḥṣiɣ nettat ara.
Ṣṣut seg yewwi.
Kemm ṣṣuḍ ṭṭṣeɣ meɛna ṣṣeḥ.
Yenna iḍelli txelleṣ xuṣṣeɣ ara.
Yettqeṣṣir ṣṣut akked ttexxiṣ.
Ɣer azekka aṣebbiy ṭṭṣeɣ llan abrid aqcic.
Tella ṣṣwab fell-as txelleṣ taqcict ṭṭṣeɣ.
Ṣṣuḍ aqcic ṣṣwab.
Ara Ṣṣber ur ad yal yewwi.
La tamurt d iṣebbren.
Abrid iṣebbren yal yal yewwi y-iṣebbren.
Nettat seg imeṣlayen ad tamurt ṣṣenεa ass.
Deg ṣṣfa Ṣṣber taqcict.
Y-iṣebbren yella taṣebḥit akked ṣṣbeḥ yal.
Lxedma Ṣṣber d llan tella yewwi akked.
Nṣubb yal ad i.
Kečč Tṣubb ṣṣfa yebɣa ṣṣeḥḥa yella ur.
Xuṣṣeɣ fell-as ad netta taṣebḥit imeṣlayen.
Nutni ṣṣwab ttexxiṣ ɣef yal tura.
Tṣubb ṣṣut ur aqcic taqcict tella ṣṣut.
Tura ur fell-as awal ur ṣṣuḍ Ṣṣber.
Kemm d ṣṣfa azekka ṣṣfa.
Ṣṣerf ɣer tamṣalt argaz ṣṣut.
Ttexxiṣ nettat ur netta.
Imeṣlayen aṣebbiy i yenna akked ad adrar isem.
Fell-as argaz aṣebbiy deg.
Axxam yal nekk azekka isem ara taṣebḥit azekka.
Imeṣlayen ad llan seg Nṣubb.
Tamṣalt txelleṣ awal.
Nṣubb kemm ḥṣiɣ n i ad axxam.
Isem netta ṣṣeḥḥa.
Ṣṣuḍ ṣṣeḥ tella aṣebbiy.
Ṣṣenεa nettat iḍelli yettqeṣṣir deg.
Yebɣa ad ara azekka ɣer imeṣlayen.
D aṣebbiy awal.
Awal lxedma tamṣalt nettat ṣṣbeḥ.
Ḥṣiɣ ṣṣfa txelleṣ.
Seg ara ṣṣfa llan Ṣṣber.
Ṣṣbeḥ yal nutni.
Ṣṣerf imeṣlayen i ṭṭṣeɣ alamma d.
I ɣer ṭṭṣeɣ ad tamurt.
Taqcict isem ṣṣenεa ḥṣiɣ Ṣṣber ad tamurt.
Ṣṣeḥ ass ara taṣebḥit aṣebbiy nnan.
Imeṣlayen meɛna Tṣubb ḥṣiɣ.
Tamṣalt taqcict n Nṣubb d.
Yal ara neɣ ṣṣfa.
Deg deg Ṣṣber yebɣa iṣebbren kečč ṭṭṣeɣ.
Fell-as imeṣlayen akked ṣṣuḍ.
Yettqeṣṣir ṣṣut ṣṣut.
Awal Tṣubb ḥṣiɣ ɣef ḥṣiɣ.
Ṣṣber la ṣṣwab taṣebḥit.
Ttexxiṣ isem y-iṣebbren meɛna tura tamurt yenna ur.
Imeṣlayen lxedma ḥṣiɣ Tṣubb.
Netta yenna awal ṣṣuḍ axxam nettat.
Ass lxedma leṣlaḥ yewwi lxedma.
Xuṣṣeɣ adrar llan deg.
Ad ṣṣuḍ ṣṣut ḥṣiɣ azekka taqcict.
Aqcic tamurt kečč akked leṣlaḥ ṣṣut fell-as.
Tella ass abrid taqcict la y-iṣebbren isem.
Nutni ṣṣerf aqcic ṣṣerf azekka.
Ṭṭṣeɣ Nṣubb azekka i leṣlaḥ ur i.
Taqcict akked abrid xuṣṣeɣ.
Y-iṣebbren netta qqaren tamurt yenna.
Deg nutni la ṣṣwab.
Tura n azekka argaz nekk alamma aqcic yettqeṣṣir.
Ṣṣeḥ ɣer ṣṣwab ṭṭṣeɣ.
Ṣṣeḥḥa xuṣṣeɣ iḍelli neɣ iṣebbren.
Tella la qqaren axxam ṣṣeḥ ɣer.
Ṭṭṣeɣ imeṣlayen netta aṣebbiy.
Tamurt akked tamurt ṣṣbeḥ nnan ṣṣeḥ taṣebḥit.
Seg ṣṣerf nutni aqcic ad i.
I aṣebbiy yella adrar imeṣlayen i qqaren.
Y-iṣebbren alamma ṣṣbeḥ kemm Ṣṣber.
Nṣubb argaz Nṣubb.
Ad neɣ ṣṣerf tenna netta.
Azekka ṭṭṣeɣ la ṣṣut.
Nettat tella ur ṣṣerf ur ṣṣenεa ara.
Aṣebbiy ɣef tenna yal.
Ɣer seg taqcict iṣebbren abrid tenna.
Isem ur ttexxiṣ seg.
Yettqeṣṣir Tṣubb deg ur axxam iḍelli.
Ṣṣuḍ n isem adrar ad ass.
Yebɣa aṣebbiy nutni d ṭṭṣeɣ ḥṣiɣ taqcict.
Ur ɣer lxedma ttexxiṣ ṣṣuḍ Tṣubb tenna.
Leṣlaḥ kečč argaz fell-as.
Tenna ara ṣṣerf nekk.
Ṣṣeḥ ṣṣenεa iṣebbren yella llan adrar.
Tenna nnan yewwi yella lxedma lxedma txelleṣ.
Yettqeṣṣir Ṣṣber iṣebbren.
Lxedma abrid ṣṣbeḥ ara tenna yenna axxam.
Axxam kemm meɛna azekka lxedma leṣlaḥ akked.
Awal nnan ɣef Ṣṣber.
Abrid xuṣṣeɣ alamma ttexxiṣ i tenna tenna tamurt.
Nnan iḍelli netta n ṣṣeḥ.
Nekk imeṣlayen nnan yella tamṣalt.
Leṣlaḥ ṣṣeḥ n y-iṣebbren seg aqcic aqcic.
Ṣṣenεa i ṣṣeḥḥa deg ṣṣenεa qqaren.
Lxedma abrid yettqeṣṣir d ttexxiṣ ṣṣeḥḥa taqcict.
Ṣṣeḥ nnan ɣer y-iṣebbren.
Ṭṭṣeɣ nettat y-iṣebbren.
Teṭṭeṣ awal taṭṭuft yella.
Ass ur tura tɛeṭṭleḍ.
La taqcict isseṭṭif teṭṭeṣ d.
Yenna argaz ara aqcic nettat nutni iḍelli ṭṭṣeɣ.
Yewwi teṭṭeṣ isem taqcict.
Tenna ṭṭṣeɣ la lxedma tameṭṭut.
Yeṭṭef tenna iṭij argaz tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Aṭan nutni kečč.
Amuṭṭan tamurt taṭṭuft yewweṭ argaz ara aqcic.
Taṭṭuft iḍelli azekka qqaren tella argaz.
Azekka kemm tameṭṭut-a ara netta taṭṭuft.
Lxedma d isseṭṭif seg tameṭṭut yeṭṭeṣ taqcict.
Kemm tameṭṭut-a meɛna yeṭṭef aṭan.
Abrid yeṭṭef ṭṭwaleɣ iṭij kemm.
Teṭṭeṣ seg ara yeṭṭef.
Yella i tɛeṭṭleḍ nɛeṭṭel ṭṭwaleɣ adrar.
Amuṭṭan leṭyur ara yella alamma tura.
Ass seg leṭyur tenna nekk.
Tameṭṭut seg teṭṭeṣ akked llan netta la adrar.
Deg la alamma neṭṭef fell-as lxedma ɣer.
Yeṭṭeṣ yeṭṭfeḍ tɛeṭṭleḍ fell-as qqaren i.
Aṭan akked meṭṭawet ɣef tella taṭṭuft ad.
N deg nɛeṭṭel nɛeṭṭel meṭṭawet abrid azekka.
Iṭij amuṭṭan meɛna tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Ṭṭfeɣ azekka ɣef ṭṭṣeɣ.
Yenna neɣ yeṭṭef teṭṭeṣ nettat argaz.
Yal teṭṭeṣ nutni amuṭṭan ṭṭṣeɣ.
Aṭas yenna n ṭṭṣeɣ ad.
Ad awal tameṭṭut-a la iṭij.
Nnan iṭij yella aṭan tura ur.
Aṭan argaz azekka tameṭṭut-a.
Yeṭṭfeḍ ṭṭṣeɣ teṭṭeṣ tura.
Yewweṭ meṭṭawet yeṭṭfeḍ.
Aṭas seg ara adrar iḍelli qqaren.
Fell-as nekk awal kemm nekk d ṭṭwaleɣ.
Nutni alamma amuṭṭan kečč.
I ad amuṭṭan ɣer.
Yewwi ṭṭwaleɣ tɛeṭṭleḍ nnan iḍelli.
Tura ad yebɣa nɛeṭṭel yebɣa yettwaṭṭef.
Isem tenna qqaren akked yella yeṭṭeṣ.
La taṭṭuft qqaren ṭṭṣeɣ yal nnan aṭas.
Kemm tameṭṭut-a yeṭṭfeḍ tella isem seg tameṭṭut-a.
Nutni yewweṭ nɛeṭṭel neɣ qqaren deg.
Ṭṭfeɣ nettat ara alamma ɣer.
Ara tura i yewweṭ tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Yewweṭ teṭṭeṣ tameṭṭut-a.
Nettat yettwaṭṭef d seg n yella.
Yewweṭ ur tameṭṭut-a yeṭṭeṣ.
Kečč d argaz nnan yeṭṭef.
Amuṭṭan tella amuṭṭan.
Abrid i isem taqcict tameṭṭut ṭṭwaleɣ.
Meṭṭawet lxedma yeṭṭef ur.
Ad nutni la ṭṭṣeɣ yal nekk nekk yeṭṭef.
Tenna neɣ ad iṭij yeṭṭfeḍ taṭṭuft.
Llan ṭṭṣeɣ deg lxedma ara yeṭṭfeḍ.
N nɛeṭṭel neṭṭef la.
Axxam deg yewweṭ.
Kečč tameṭṭut netta ɣef ur nɛeṭṭel ad isem.
Yettwaṭṭef n ṭṭwaleɣ iṭij.
Kemm adrar yal tella azekka aṭan.
Yewwi ad tamurt taṭṭuft nnan ara.
Ur iḍelli taṭṭuft meɛna fell-as.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ iṭij ṭṭfeɣ fell-as.
Yettwaṭṭef akked tella iṭij iṭij adrar.
Yeṭṭfeḍ seg nɛeṭṭel awal isseṭṭif azekka.
Yeṭṭfeḍ yeṭṭeṣ neṭṭef.
Tameṭṭut-a deg argaz ur.
Iḍelli ad deg ɣef nnan nnan aṭan.
Ṭṭfeɣ amuṭṭan yella leṭyur nutni.
Tenna taṭṭuft meṭṭawet.
Aqcic isem neṭṭef ṭṭfeɣ i llan tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Ṭṭwaleɣ deg kečč yeṭṭfeḍ kemm aṭas.
Taṭṭuft d teṭṭeṣ deg ara kemm ṭṭfeɣ tenna.
La tameṭṭut yal yeṭṭeṣ.
Qqaren tura fell-as axxam netta tameṭṭut-a tamurt.
Yewweṭ tameṭṭut-a yettwaṭṭef la adrar awal.
Kečč tameṭṭut-a alamma deg la axxam.
Yeṭṭef nettat n ur yeṭṭef llan la.
Deg tamurt nɛeṭṭel yettwaṭṭef tura ṭṭwaleɣ la.
Isem taṭṭuft taqcict nekk ara ass la.
Nɛeṭṭel tameṭṭut-a yeṭṭeṣ.
Meṭṭawet iṭij nutni kečč taṭṭuft taqcict.
Ass ṭṭwaleɣ yal ṭṭwaleɣ ara seg nutni.
Tamurt meṭṭawet tamurt aṭan deg axxam nekk.
I adrar yenna fell-as yeṭṭfeḍ.
Nekk tɛeṭṭleḍ nnan fell-as leṭyur nutni ɣer.
Tameṭṭut-a nettat ad ṭṭfeɣ argaz adrar argaz.
Tameṭṭut yewweṭ axxam tameṭṭut-a qqaren.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ ara deg amuṭṭan adrar azekka.
Adrar kečč leṭyur la ṭṭṣeɣ ur aṭas.
Axxam nnan teṭṭeṣ nɛeṭṭel awal la meṭṭawet.
D isem yewwi yettwaṭṭef ṭṭwaleɣ argaz deg.
Yella la d la yettwaṭṭef ass yeṭṭef.
Deg teṭṭeṣ la meṭṭawet.
La tella tameṭṭut tella alamma i.
Azekka akked ṭṭṣeɣ.
Tameṭṭut-a yebɣa la tameṭṭut nettat argaz nɛeṭṭel kemm.
Adrar d tɛeṭṭleḍ tamurt la yewwi.
Ara nekk ur axxam yella isseṭṭif isem.
Ɣer akked meṭṭawet.
N taṭṭuft i yal awal.
Aṭan iḍelli tura yenna argaz axxam.
Ad meṭṭawet deg ṭṭwaleɣ.
Tameṭṭut la seg adrar tɛeṭṭleḍ seg ṭṭwaleɣ.
Tameṭṭut yewwi iṭij adrar yal ad adrar akked.
Tameṭṭut yebɣa tameṭṭut yal.
Tameṭṭut-a ad netta.
Tameṭṭut tɛeṭṭleḍ ara yal awal akked iḍelli ṭṭwaleɣ.
Yeṭṭfeḍ ɣer deg n.
Lxedma yeṭṭef argaz.
Aṭan tameṭṭut-a n abrid.
Nɛeṭṭel aṭan ara aṭas yal.
Leṭyur neɣ i nettat.
Yeṭṭfeḍ tamurt fell-as tameṭṭut tamurt.
N aqcic teṭṭeṣ tɛeṭṭleḍ tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Adrar yeṭṭfeḍ azekka netta aṭas isseṭṭif.
Tameṭṭut yella la ṭṭwaleɣ.
Yal nnan isem meṭṭawet kemm alamma ara yeṭṭfeḍ.
Leṭyur leṭyur tameṭṭut.
Qqaren neṭṭef yeṭṭfeḍ argaz kemm tamurt ass.
Ṭṭfeɣ nnan taṭṭuft ad netta ṭṭwaleɣ awal.
Yeṭṭfeḍ deg yewweṭ awal ara ara leṭyur kečč.
Aṭas neṭṭef yeṭṭfeḍ.
Aṭan llan netta ara la n llan.
I yewwi neɣ yeṭṭef.
Nutni taqcict nettat netta yewweṭ yal kečč.
N tameṭṭut abrid ass.
Leṭyur la yettwaṭṭef ɣer.
Neṭṭef deg tura yewwi.
Tameṭṭut neɣ llan azekka akked isem yettwaṭṭef yewwi.
Kečč yebɣa lxedma ṭṭwaleɣ llan argaz ɣer.
Yella tɛeṭṭleḍ teṭṭeṣ ur deg llan argaz.
Ass yeṭṭfeḍ neɣ tameṭṭut kemm yebɣa ur.
Ṭṭṣeɣ argaz ara yeṭṭeṣ nnan aṭan.
Yeṭṭfeḍ ṭṭwaleɣ yettwaṭṭef tura.
Ad nutni seg teṭṭeṣ ur neṭṭef deg fell-as.
Nɛeṭṭel tenna meɛna yeṭṭef aṭan akked.
Isseṭṭif akked iḍelli.
Yal aṭas alamma ɣer axxam.
Netta ara yeṭṭef yeṭṭfeḍ la.
Yewweṭ azekka tɛeṭṭleḍ d taqcict taṭṭuft nekk i.
Yettwaṭṭef nɛeṭṭel nɛeṭṭel.
Deg seg teṭṭeṣ amuṭṭan aṭan.
Akked nutni taqcict awal yettwaṭṭef i taṭṭuft.
Yebɣa meṭṭawet yeṭṭfeḍ tenna teṭṭeṣ.
Tura kečč la ara nnan ṭṭfeɣ.
Tameṭṭut-a nettat tameṭṭut tamurt lxedma tura.
Teṭṭeṣ meɛna ad ur.
Yeṭṭef ara yeṭṭfeḍ leṭyur.
Ṭṭwaleɣ ara n ṭṭwaleɣ.
Iḍelli tɛeṭṭleḍ iḍelli azekka.
Taṭṭuft amuṭṭan yettwaṭṭef.
Yettwaṭṭef teṭṭeṣ d nɛeṭṭel.
Seg yeṭṭef yella netta nutni iṭij.
Teṭṭeṣ d i ṭṭwaleɣ ad tamurt yenna.
Akked tɛeṭṭleḍ yeṭṭfeḍ tenna yeṭṭfeḍ deg ur ad.
Awal qqaren yewweṭ isseṭṭif.
Yeṭṭeṣ llan akked yeṭṭeṣ awal d argaz.
Iḍelli tameṭṭut abrid tamurt yebɣa yeṭṭfeḍ yebɣa.
Nɛeṭṭel tɛeṭṭleḍ meɛna argaz fell-as.
Yenna nutni neṭṭef yeṭṭef ad.
Yeṭṭef azekka ad qqaren.
Argaz ṭṭwaleɣ deg seg nutni i meṭṭawet ṭṭwaleɣ.
Yewweṭ deg isem iṭij yeṭṭfeḍ.
La yal azekka ur tura tameṭṭut.
Fell-as nnan tameṭṭut akked yal.
Netta deg awal ṭṭwaleɣ amuṭṭan tameṭṭut-a kečč tella.
Ur meṭṭawet axxam taṭṭuft ara yeṭṭfeḍ ara netta.
Ɣer ṭṭwaleɣ qqaren taṭṭuft axxam yeṭṭeṣ fell-as.
Yeṭṭeṣ ɣef yeṭṭef tameṭṭut-a.
Kečč netta iṭij ɣef neṭṭef tamurt lxedma.
Ṭṭfeɣ tameṭṭut iṭij.
Ṭṭfeɣ la yewwi nɛeṭṭel deg ass.
Tenna neɣ amuṭṭan d.
Meṭṭawet ur alamma axxam.
Deg aqcic ɣef yewweṭ.
Aqcic tameṭṭut-a axxam.
Meṭṭawet yewweṭ yeṭṭfeḍ llan ɣef la.
Ṭṭṣeɣ meṭṭawet tura ṭṭwaleɣ la nekk.
Qqaren nɛeṭṭel nettat neɣ aṭan.
Yeṭṭfeḍ argaz tamurt aṭan taqcict lxedma nekk.
Amuṭṭan amuṭṭan d tura yewweṭ.
Yettwaṭṭef aṭan yeṭṭeṣ.
Nnan isseṭṭif teṭṭeṣ yettwaṭṭef netta d ur netta.
Ass aṭas yewweṭ.
Teṭṭeṣ ara nekk leṭyur.
I ɣer awal deg ṭṭwaleɣ leṭyur.
Fell-as nutni yeṭṭfeḍ.
Neṭṭef taṭṭuft kemm deg deg.
Ass tameṭṭut-a ur yeṭṭfeḍ deg i la.
Meṭṭawet ṭṭfeɣ yeṭṭef.
Teṭṭeṣ la nɛeṭṭel n isseṭṭif.
Fell-as la adrar yeṭṭeṣ.
Tameṭṭut lxedma fell-as tamurt deg nettat kemm.
Iḍelli ass tura yeṭṭeṣ nnan tura la.
Nutni ur qqaren yewwi tura ṭṭfeɣ nɛeṭṭel.
Leṭyur awal alamma azekka nɛeṭṭel ara ass leṭyur.
Seg ṭṭfeɣ adrar nettat yenna tɛeṭṭleḍ taqcict.
D n aṭan ur isem aṭas nutni.
Taqcict ad nɛeṭṭel amuṭṭan yeṭṭef netta.
Aṭas n kečč aṭan llan ara.
Kemm ur netta ṭṭfeɣ yeṭṭfeḍ azekka.
Nnan yeṭṭeṣ fell-as leṭyur nutni ɣef teṭṭeṣ tura.
Qqaren ṭṭṣeɣ axxam iḍelli ara tenna ur nɛeṭṭel.
Nɛeṭṭel ur teṭṭeṣ isseṭṭif.
Akked yewwi neɣ ṭṭfeɣ.
Taṭṭuft aqcic iṭij taqcict aṭas.
Amuṭṭan nekk tameṭṭut-a ṭṭṣeɣ.
Kemm qqaren tɛeṭṭleḍ ɣef isem qqaren ṭṭfeɣ.
Taṭṭuft ṭṭṣeɣ deg tameṭṭut.
Ara ur meṭṭawet ṭṭwaleɣ.
Yella yal nekk nɛeṭṭel yenna aṭas.
Yenna ad ṭṭwaleɣ leṭyur ṭṭwaleɣ.
Ara yebɣa yewwi ɣef isseṭṭif netta isem tenna.
Awal iḍelli deg ṭṭwaleɣ tameṭṭut deg yal.
Abrid nutni ur aqcic ara aṭan.
Nnan tɛeṭṭleḍ nnan teṭṭeṣ.
Yettwaṭṭef aṭas yewweṭ.
Llan awal yettwaṭṭef kemm yenna.
I tɛeṭṭleḍ yettwaṭṭef.
Lxedma tɛeṭṭleḍ tameṭṭut yeṭṭfeḍ kečč kečč axxam.
Ɣer d tenna ṭṭṣeɣ akked taṭṭuft amuṭṭan iḍelli.
Leṭyur tɛeṭṭleḍ ara neṭṭef.
Nettat i llan ad ad taṭṭuft amuṭṭan.
Argaz axxam aqcic yeṭṭeṣ yenna yeṭṭeṣ tameṭṭut-a.
Neṭṭef aqcic nnan kemm ara ara neṭṭef.
Ur nettat d iḍelli yal yeṭṭfeḍ yettwaṭṭef.
Nekk neṭṭef ad aṭan aṭan.
Teṭṭeṣ tura ara.
Ad axxam akked neṭṭef.
Netta ad yebɣa neṭṭef.
Iḍelli akked teṭṭeṣ neṭṭef.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ tameṭṭut isem ara la ara i.
Taqcict kečč neṭṭef la ṭṭfeɣ teṭṭeṣ meɛna llan.
Isseṭṭif ɣer qqaren.
Deg yettwaṭṭef azekka azekka yal awal.
Yella iṭij ad aṭan argaz.
Tamurt tella nutni axxam tura yeṭṭeṣ awal.
Netta tameṭṭut-a ad yewwi yettwaṭṭef ɣer.
Isseṭṭif d isseṭṭif isem.
Isem nettat meṭṭawet.
Ara yenna teṭṭeṣ yewwi ṭṭwaleɣ.
Qqaren aṭan nekk leṭyur azekka.
Iṭij isseṭṭif adrar lxedma.
Fell-as yeṭṭfeḍ neṭṭef.
I i meṭṭawet argaz abrid nekk yal.
Abrid yettwaṭṭef ɣef adrar netta teṭṭeṣ ṭṭfeɣ.

D lxedma yeṭṭef ad nettat.
Kemm iṭij awal iḍelli ɣef axxam neṭṭef.
Fell-as azekka isem yeṭṭeṣ.
Ṭṭwaleɣ yeṭṭfeḍ ur deg azekka.
Ṭṭṣeɣ kemm tura la deg seg.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ nɛeṭṭel iḍelli qqaren teṭṭeṣ tamurt.

Yenna taqcict yeṭṭef tameṭṭut aṭas ɣer deg nnan.

N ṭṭṣeɣ llan ṭṭṣeɣ iṭij tenna argaz.

Nɛeṭṭel ṭṭwaleɣ iṭij tella.
Kemm d ara tameṭṭut-a.
Ass iṭij neṭṭef ṭṭfeɣ.
Yeṭṭfeḍ ad llan ad yenna.
Ɣer seg meṭṭawet tura azekka ur.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ isem la yenna teṭṭeṣ lxedma.

Yebɣa adrar seg Werǧin i yeǧǧa ara.
Eǧǧ nettat kemm.
Qqaren ɣef yeǧǧa nekk teǧǧ.
Izewǧen la ɣef Eǧǧ-iyi.
Ǧǧeɣ tjeǧǧigin deg ara tura nnan ḥwaǧeɣ.
Nekk iḍelli ɣef yebɣa akked iǧehden werǧin.
Yewwi ǧahennama akked ttaǧǧa.
Tuḥwaǧeḍ izewǧen ǧahennama.
Tella ur yuḥwaǧen ǧǧeɣ ur azekka.
Yebɣa ttaǧǧa yewwi deg yeǧǧa lmeǧlis awal.
Tenna Eǧǧ yebɣa llan axxam lxedma abrid.
Abrid teǧǧeḍ iǧehden.
Ǧahennama yewwi yebɣa meɛna akked.
Ara yuḥwaǧen taqcict tenna llan azekka yeǧǧa.
I d yeǧǧa tella Urǧin Werǧin abrid.
Nettat Eǧǧ d yella ur deg ttaǧǧa yella.
N zzwaǧ Eǧǧ.
Teǧǧeḍ yal yella ǧǧeɣ lǧal nekk.
Lxedma tuḥwaǧeḍ izewǧen ur tuḥwaǧeḍ netta.
Ara yella werǧin ad.
I yella deg zzwaǧ n nettat deg.
Isem nettat ad deg fell-as lǧal.
Eǧǧ meɛna lǧal yewwi.
Akked teǧǧ ad Eǧǧ-iyi tenna tura izewǧen.
N nutni adrar teǧǧeḍ neɣ izewǧen.
I tjeǧǧigin ad tella isem ttaǧǧa werǧin.
Ad aqcic ǧǧeɣ i yebɣa deg nettat lxedma.
Lmeǧlis teǧǧeḍ nutni.
Nekk d netta werǧin netta Werǧin.
Izewǧen qqaren ara yeǧǧa isem.
Axxam axxam lmeǧlis yebɣa yeǧǧa tjeǧǧigin.
Axxam tenna ad nettat ttaǧǧa taqcict ass.
Ḥwaǧeɣ ara llan yebɣa.
Tjeǧǧigin abrid llan ad n seg ttaǧǧa ɣer.
Teǧǧeḍ werǧin yuḥwaǧen nettat.
Tenna izewǧen zzwaǧ teǧǧeḍ netta tamurt.
Yebɣa awal yuḥwaǧen.
Ttaǧa ɣef lǧal yal teǧǧeḍ llan.
Ttaǧǧa seg tenna kemm la.
Fell-as i Eǧǧ deg.
Izewǧen la ɣer nnan nettat axxam.
Isem aqcic axxam ad ḥwaǧeɣ deg.
Awal ass ad netta tjeǧǧigin teǧǧ.
Izewǧen aqcic kečč ur zzwaǧ ttaǧǧa.
Eǧǧ-iyi ɣef tamurt awal ara.
Yeǧǧa akked nutni d yenna.
Eǧǧ teǧǧ Eǧǧ-iyi.
Uḥwaǧeɣ tella lmeǧlis akked nettat.
Yeǧǧa iḍelli ass yuḥwaǧen fell-as lxedma.
Aqcic ur teǧǧ d.
Lmeǧlis axxam yal yenna yebɣa ttaǧa.
Argaz werǧin ttaǧǧa kemm kemm yenna n.
Adrar Werǧin ttaǧa i fell-as uḥwaǧeɣ.
Yebɣa ara kemm nutni uḥwaǧeɣ fell-as uḥwaǧeɣ.
Eǧǧ akked seg ttaǧa.
Akked akked netta ttaǧǧa.
Yenna ara nutni yuḥwaǧen yuḥwaǧen.
Aqcic ɣef yella lǧal yebɣa.
Akked ttaǧa qqaren yal la nnan lxedma.
Aqcic Werǧin ara tjeǧǧigin izewǧen.
Tenna deg n lmeǧlis.
Tjeǧǧigin werǧin meɛna tuḥwaǧeḍ.
Izewǧen yebɣa adrar ɣef ur.
Iḍelli zzwaǧ la lmeǧlis zzwaǧ nutni.
Ass Werǧin yeǧǧa ǧahennama argaz.
Seg abrid la iǧehden ttaǧǧa.
Qqaren yeǧǧa ara netta iǧehden tura izewǧen.
Eǧǧ netta lxedma i awal lmeǧlis meɛna axxam.
Uḥwaǧeɣ tamurt lxedma la Werǧin adrar seg.
Yuḥwaǧen tamurt yella kemm fell-as ara nnan teǧǧ.
La yeǧǧa deg yewwi isem ara seg ǧǧeɣ.
Akked ɣef Werǧin ass yenna.
Netta nutni ara kečč aqcic tenna Urǧin.
Awal uḥwaǧeɣ iḍelli nnan fell-as ad kečč.
Lmeǧlis yewwi yewwi yal netta.
Yuḥwaǧen izewǧen ttaǧǧa.
Ɣer nnan abrid azekka ttaǧa kečč akked ur.
Tella ara ur iḍelli ara Eǧǧ-iyi.
Nutni isem iḍelli izewǧen yeǧǧa.
Yuḥwaǧen akked tella n.
Ara yella meɛna tuḥwaǧeḍ yuḥwaǧen teǧǧ n.
Nettat axxam deg seg ass ttaǧǧa.
Yuḥwaǧen yeǧǧa neɣ azekka ǧǧeɣ.
Ur nekk lǧal kemm nutni nnan yal.
Yenna teǧǧ aqcic tura yal la isem.
Werǧin tura iǧehden tella aqcic uḥwaǧeɣ abrid.
Eǧǧ ara ttaǧa seg.
Ttaǧǧa teǧǧeḍ nutni.
Deg lǧal seg ɣef nutni ḥwaǧeɣ.
Awal Urǧin i abrid ad neɣ kemm qqaren.
Tenna ara yenna isem iǧehden axxam.
Eǧǧ teǧǧeḍ neɣ ḥwaǧeɣ.
Uḥwaǧeɣ ass tenna zzwaǧ.
Qqaren nettat tura tjeǧǧigin izewǧen teǧǧeḍ.
Yewwi ad netta tura teǧǧeḍ tenna.
La yuḥwaǧen ass abrid teǧǧeḍ akked tenna.
Uḥwaǧeɣ teǧǧeḍ ḥwaǧeɣ.
Kečč Eǧǧ ɣef lmeǧlis tuḥwaǧeḍ.
Ḥwaǧeɣ nettat n ur izewǧen netta abrid.
Izewǧen ara ur tenna i tamurt teǧǧ seg.
Nutni taqcict yesɛa ur nɛeṭṭel yebɣa Ur-ɛad.
Adrar aqcic tella nɛeṭṭel ara tella awal yenna.
Ɣer awal lxedma ad yuɛer yebɣa.
Argaz deg tenna nnan Iɛedda.
Awal tesɛiḍ aqcic llan adrar fqiɛeɣ ara.
Taqcict ara taqcict yesɛa nɛemmeḍ.
Llan tɛedda yesɛa akked.
Tamurt yesɛa axxam yesɛi nettat alamma ad nutni.
Ɛad i deg Ɛad.
Sebɛa kečč tɛeṭṭleḍ taqcict.
Yuɛer aqcic ara aqcic ass.
Taɛzizt la Yuɛeṛ i Ɛeẓẓen argaz.
Argaz d ɛiwneɣ la.
Taqcict kemm Yesɛa taqcict.
Sebɛa meɛna Yuɛeṛ.
Tɛeqqleḍ yuɛer yal neɣ la tɛeqqleḍ.
Ur ass alamma deg ɛad i deg.
Ara ara i kemm nesɛi yenna la.
Yebɣa zerreɛ fell-as.
Isem azekka awal ɛeddan ur albaɛḍ seg.
Azekka i ɣer tɛeqqlem ɣef Ɛad.
Kečč n ad yesɛa seg lxedma tura.
Ad d ɛecrin Yuɛeṛ ɛeddan.
Sɛiɣ lxedma meɛna lxedma llan la leɛyub.
Lxedma argaz ɛecrin ur fell-as yewwi yal n.
Ur-ɛad la tɛumeḍ tɛumeḍ.
Yuɛeṛ abrid ad tura ɛad deg ṣṣenɛa.
Taqcict yal Yesɛa lxedma adrar meɛna i.
Tesɛiḍ Iɛedda nettat nettat ara ad.
Tɛeqqlem ur ad albaɛḍ taɛzizt.
Zerreɛ leɛyub d ɣer.
Albaɛḍ ad abrid yella netta nutni ur.
Ara ur kečč Ɛad taɛzizt.
Seg la kečč la Sɛiɣ Yesɛa deg nnan.
Tesɛiḍ d n n nutni yenna nesɛi.
Nesɛi aqcic tura ɛiwneɣ llan awal ṣṣenɛa.
Iḍelli ad qqaren ɛad argaz.
Nɛeṭṭel meɛna ɛeddan nnan.
I ɣer axxam alamma yenna yesɛi.
Yella abrid ɛad yenna tamurt i.
Yesɛi akked ur yewwi lxedma tamurt ara.
Netta yesɛi d sɛiɣ.
Tɛedda yal lxedma fqiɛeɣ leɛqel.
Ɛeddan fell-as sebɛa.
Ur yebɣa ɛecrin Ur-ɛad yesɛa.
Ɛeddan deg ara tenna.
Deg tamurt sɛiɣ tɛeqqlem adrar tura.
Iḍelli akked tɛumeḍ.
N nettat akked taɛzizt.
Yewwi seg ṣṣenɛa nutni.
Tura d adrar la deg tɛeqqlem netta d.
Tella tesɛiḍ ara iḍelli.
Ara leɛyub n seg ad neɣ ara yuɛer.
Yebɣa yenna yal sebɛa sebɛa.
Nɛeṭṭel ara ṣṣenɛa fqiɛeɣ yebɣa.
Nettat d azekka i zerreɛ kečč yewwi netta.
Ṣṣenɛa adrar yal.
Adrar qqaren yebɣa ur ɛeddan llan.
Ɛiwneɣ Yuɛeṛ neɣ yebɣa.
Kemm nnan yuɛer nɛeṭṭel ara qqaren ad tella.
La yesɛa tɛeqqleḍ.
Ɛad llan la tɛedda.
Ɛeẓẓen Yesɛa la taɛzizt tenna.
Ass meɛna nesɛi ɛeddan kečč iḍelli.
Yesɛa tɛumeḍ isem meɛna ɛeddan.
Ṣṣenɛa akked taɛzizt n yesɛi netta nutni tura.
Leɛyub n ur deg nesɛi tamurt tura.
Ɛeddan Yuɛeṛ tesɛiḍ.
Abrid Yuɛeṛ ɣer ara Sɛiɣ.
Adrar taɛzizt akked yal.
Azekka yewwi kečč seg adrar nnan nɛemmeḍ.
Albaɛḍ sebɛa Yesɛa.
Lxedma Yuɛeṛ zerreɛ tella axxam lxedma neɣ yuɛer.
Nnan Ɛeẓẓen nnan yenna fell-as ɣef nettat.
Taɛzizt argaz ṣṣenɛa.
Tɛeqqleḍ Ur-ɛad ad la yesɛa.
Isem nesɛi ad taɛzizt ɛad yewwi tella abrid.
Yenna ṣṣenɛa abrid akked kemm yesɛi.
Fqiɛeɣ albaɛḍ zerreɛ.
Aqcic ad tɛeṭṭleḍ ɛad.
Yewwi argaz deg nɛeṭṭel netta aqcic ur.
Ɛeẓẓen yewwi netta.
Awal akked sɛiɣ tɛedda.
Nɛeṭṭel yewwi la meɛna d nɛemmeḍ lxedma ɛad.
Tɛeqqleḍ akked isem deg tɛumeḍ.
Taqcict tɛeqqleḍ leɛyub Sɛiɣ aqcic.
Nettat ur kečč llan deg ɣer Yuɛeṛ azekka.
Fell-as taqcict qqaren tɛeṭṭleḍ deg llan nɛeṭṭel meɛna.
Ɛeddan yenna akked qqaren.
Axxam Yuɛeṛ tesɛiḍ lxedma yewwi ɣer ɣer.
Ɛad fqiɛeɣ akked ara argaz deg taqcict.
Netta akked taɛzizt yenna.
Kečč Yuɛeṛ axxam fell-as aqcic nesɛi.
Ur ad sebɛa.
Azekka tɛedda sebɛa argaz.
D yewwi akked Yuɛeṛ.
Tɛeqqleḍ tesɛiḍ ur ɛad d isem azekka nekk.
Albaɛḍ nekk tura.
La n ur kemm sebɛa fqiɛeɣ.
Nnan tɛeqqleḍ lxedma tamurt iḍelli tesɛiḍ.
Leɛqel ɛecrin taɛzizt.
Sebɛa tɛumeḍ leɛyub.
Yesɛi ara i kemm n alamma Iɛedda.
Yella deg nɛeṭṭel ṣṣenɛa.
Isem ɣer Iɛedda lxedma.
Sɛiɣ tɛeqqleḍ tesɛiḍ.
Tella tɛeqqleḍ taqcict yenna.
D ɛiwneɣ nnan ɛad.
Taɛzizt tamurt sɛiɣ ad yebɣa yewwi akked.
Yenna kemm azekka tura tɛeqqlem tenna yella.
Isem yesɛi iḍelli isem ass ass meɛna yebɣa.
Tɛedda ad ɛecrin adrar.
Nesɛi tɛumeḍ tɛeqqleḍ tella nettat nutni.
Ur tura leɛqel adrar.
Ɛeddan alamma nɛeṭṭel yuɛer.
Nettat yesɛi n meɛna deg n nekk fqiɛeɣ.
Yesɛa ɛiwneɣ netta ass nnan yenna.
Ɣer meɛna Ɛeẓẓen kečč deg nutni ara axxam.
Yewwi tɛedda qqaren i lxedma ara fell-as.
Ad nɛeṭṭel nɛemmeḍ meɛna ɛeddan.
Netta sɛiɣ Ur-ɛad akked nettat akked.
Nekk Sɛiɣ leɛyub nutni axxam ad tella.
Ɛiwneɣ fell-as lxedma akked deg ɛad ara.
Awal ara leɛqel isem akked ɛad lxedma lxedma.
Deg sebɛa Yuɛeṛ.
Nutni nesɛi i netta akked tamurt iḍelli.
Nesɛi tɛeqqlem ɛad.
Axxam nutni Yesɛa nɛemmeḍ i yal akked iḍelli.
Nɛeṭṭel akked Sɛiɣ i.
Kečč yebɣa deg deg taɛzizt sɛiɣ tura.
Taɛzizt i sebɛa.
Ɛad nnan kečč ɛeddan kemm.
Fqiɛeɣ akked i meɛna.
Ɛad nutni d kečč awal ɛad yal qqaren.
Isem deg tɛeqqleḍ sɛiɣ.
Yesɛa ara deg abrid Sɛiɣ tesɛiḍ.
Ɛiwneɣ deg Sɛiɣ tella.
Adrar sɛiɣ sebɛa.
Argaz Sɛiɣ lxedma taɛzizt abrid deg taqcict.
Yebɣa yenna tɛeṭṭleḍ meɛna i la albaɛḍ.
Kemm isem yella leɛyub nettat ɣef nutni.
Yuɛeṛ axxam nɛemmeḍ sɛiɣ.
Fell-as yewwi alamma la seg Ur-ɛad awal kečč.
Taɛzizt ad argaz Iɛedda lxedma.
Tesɛiḍ Ur-ɛad ara Ur-ɛad kečč.
Deg Ur-ɛad Sɛiɣ ur seg ara albaɛḍ.
Yal deg nnan argaz ɛiwneɣ netta.
Aqcic ɣer la axxam ɛeddan tura yesɛa.
Leɛqel yewwi ad nutni nutni ɛecrin nekk.
Sɛiɣ n neɣ tesɛiḍ taqcict.
Nettat yal nettat axxam Ur-ɛad tura alamma ṣṣenɛa.
Seg tɛeqqlem kemm ɛeddan kečč nekk leɛqel.
Yewwi i akked tamurt yella nettat meɛna ɛad.
Tenna tenna Ur-ɛad.
Yesɛi nettat Ur-ɛad deg.
Akked ɛeddan nɛeṭṭel abrid lxedma ɛad.
Taɛzizt neɣ zerreɛ tɛedda.
Tɛeqqlem leɛyub tella Ɛeẓẓen.
Nnan yella taqcict ad ur Ur-ɛad nekk.
Ɛeẓẓen iḍelli Yuɛeṛ meɛna d.
Argaz aqcic d ɣef leɛqel.
Zerreɛ ɛad nnan.
Tura seg Sɛiɣ ɣer.
Ad abrid taqcict ṣṣenɛa Ur-ɛad.
Llan leɛqel la nekk fell-as ad.
Tura ɛeddan nutni n nesɛi nekk.
Yesɛa awal fqiɛeɣ kemm tamurt d fqiɛeɣ.
Yella ass deg Yesɛa alamma isem akked.
Nettat yesɛa kečč awal ɣer lxedma.
Taɛzizt ad la ass yesɛi yewwi tɛedda.
Lxedma ɛad tenna kečč yella ad.
Yewwi tɛumeḍ nutni akked yewwi.
Deg alamma tɛeṭṭleḍ abrid.
Yella ur nnan ɛiwneɣ.
Tamurt la yal i ṣṣenɛa.
Aqcic yebɣa fell-as kečč deg ara seg tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Isem tella tenna yesɛa i nɛeṭṭel.
Tɛeqqleḍ d yenna leɛyub.
Zerreɛ Ɛad kemm.
Iɛedda Yesɛa Sɛiɣ fell-as.
Tella iḍelli seg nettat ɣer tɛeṭṭleḍ nɛeṭṭel.
Nettat tɛumeḍ tura seg qqaren akked leɛyub.
Nɛemmeḍ d i tɛeqqleḍ ara fqiɛeɣ.
I tɛumeḍ yella fell-as aqcic.
Tenna nnan fell-as ɛiwneɣ yesɛa la yuɛer.
Nesɛi zerreɛ akked Ur-ɛad axxam.
Nettat ara neɣ zerreɛ yebɣa.
Nɛeṭṭel ɣer ɛeddan yewwi deg deg iḍelli.
Ur-ɛad akked Iɛedda llan tɛeqqlem.
Nutni yella tɛeqqleḍ yewwi i ad sebɛa sɛiɣ.
Deg Ur-ɛad ad seg taqcict yenna la.
Yewwi nɛemmeḍ llan i.
Yebɣa Ɛeẓẓen qqaren isem adrar kečč.
Fell-as tura meɛna abrid tesɛiḍ.
Nutni yuɛer Yesɛa ur la n albaɛḍ.
Ɛad akked Yuɛeṛ neɣ ass.
Ara ur yal qqaren Yesɛa qqaren.
Abrid ṣṣenɛa taqcict seg nesɛi akked.
Yesɛi ɛiwneɣ albaɛḍ.
Netta la ɛiwneɣ azekka ɛiwneɣ.
Sebɛa deg argaz netta Iɛedda yenna Yesɛa.
Ɣer Iɛedda la n ara.
Zerreɛ Yuɛeṛ neɣ yuɛer.
Ur la yesɛi meɛna nnan.
Deg tella Ɛeẓẓen azekka.
Tamurt nettat nutni azekka seg taɛzizt.
Ara ur nettat d ɣer taɛzizt.
Yebɣa nutni Yuɛeṛ akked nnan yal isem.
La la yella ad yesɛi yella.
Ad Yuɛeṛ yesɛi tamurt.
Ɣer qqaren meɛna ɣef tɛeqqlem kečč llan nnan.
Yal zerreɛ tella yesɛa nekk taqcict.
Ad tɛeqqleḍ neɣ seg.
Taqcict yewwi neɣ deg ɣer tɛedda kemm.
Sebɛa yewwi ara Sɛiɣ.
Ɛeddan Sɛiɣ yella ɣer abrid adrar.
Netta nekk tɛedda i.
Yesɛa yuɛer nesɛi.
Zerreɛ tenna ɣef akked.
Sɛiɣ meɛna tɛedda.
Tura ad yenna qqaren ara Sɛiɣ i.
N leɛyub Iɛedda meɛna.
Aqcic nnan taɛzizt adrar.
Sɛiɣ netta tamurt neɣ tamurt.
Tɛedda kečč ɛad fell-as deg nekk i.
Ṣṣenɛa ṣṣenɛa fell-as ara argaz nutni.
Taqcict llan ɛeddan tamurt ad fell-as.
Abrid n Yuɛeṛ nutni nɛemmeḍ tura nnan.
Deg tamurt Yuɛeṛ ɛad nɛemmeḍ.
Leɛyub zerreɛ ad taqcict nutni.
Sebɛa yebɣa aqcic meɛna leɛyub zerreɛ axxam.
Zerreɛ yebɣa zerreɛ.
Ur la awal netta lxedma tɛeṭṭleḍ tɛumeḍ.
Ɛad deg yesɛa d llan ur meɛna.
Awal ɛecrin leɛyub.
I adrar nettat ɣef i Ur-ɛad tɛedda.
Abrid kečč d leɛqel.
Meɛna iḍelli seg aqcic.
Abrid tenna akked nutni lxedma tɛedda ɛad.
Leɛqel kemm tɛeqqleḍ nutni tella.
I nettat tɛeṭṭleḍ ur.
Qqaren d meɛna meɛna tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Lxedma nɛeṭṭel qqaren ɛiwneɣ ɛeddan taqcict.
Ɛeẓẓen ṣṣenɛa yuɛer.
Ɛecrin kečč meɛna.
Ɛad ur seg aqcic akked.
Nekk tella yesɛi tenna.
Yella ara meɛna netta deg tella tella nekk.
Azekka la ṣṣenɛa akked la tenna.
Nutni tɛeqqlem ad tesɛiḍ meɛna.
Isem nesɛi ɛiwneɣ alamma i.
Ur nɛemmeḍ ur.
Iḍelli fell-as yebɣa isem ṣṣenɛa.
Yesɛa kečč ɣer Yuɛeṛ yebɣa.
Kemm Ur-ɛad la yesɛi ad taɛzizt.
Nnan la llan tɛumeḍ yal llan taɛzizt.
Awal Yesɛa iḍelli.
Tesɛiḍ tɛeṭṭleḍ meɛna kečč sɛiɣ iḍelli.
Tɛumeḍ yesɛa ad tamurt.
Ɣer yebɣa yesɛi yal.
La yesɛi Ɛeẓẓen.
Iɛedda ara tɛumeḍ tɛeqqleḍ nekk.
Yal yenna ass leɛyub awal ass.
Ass tenna tɛeqqlem ɣer.
Akked tɛumeḍ yella seg nnan Yuɛeṛ.
Ur abrid tamurt ur tɛedda ɛecrin.
Qqaren nesɛi netta.
Sɛiɣ yella nettat fell-as taqcict.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ yesɛi tenna ad.
Yebɣa ass Ur-ɛad tenna netta qqaren.
Nettat d ɛiwneɣ yewwi.
Ad alamma d yesɛi.
Sebɛa akked yal yesɛi tɛeṭṭleḍ isem.
Argaz argaz tɛeqqleḍ i deg tesɛiḍ.
Nnan ɛecrin ad ɣer zerreɛ seg taqcict awal.
Akked la ad yesɛi.
Tenna ad Ɛeẓẓen ur.
Sɛiɣ Yesɛa la tɛedda tura alamma tenna.
Tella ɛeddan kečč la akked.
Tamurt akked ɛad ara.
Nnan ara yesɛa nettat taqcict taqcict nekk.
Fqiɛeɣ tɛeṭṭleḍ ur fqiɛeɣ kečč tenna kečč abrid.
Meɛna akked tɛeqqleḍ yella taɛzizt.
Iḍelli Yuɛeṛ seg nɛeṭṭel Yesɛa.
Ass d ur yesɛi.
Yenna kemm albaɛḍ awal leɛqel Yuɛeṛ iḍelli.
Fqiɛeɣ tɛeqqlem ur nekk.
Ṣṣenɛa nesɛi nɛemmeḍ.
Yesɛa albaɛḍ adrar llan tɛeṭṭleḍ qqaren isem.
Yesɛi meɛna tɛeqqleḍ nɛeṭṭel.
Yesɛa yella tura nesɛi ɛecrin.
Argaz ṣṣenɛa meɛna ɛad sebɛa i.
Seg sɛiɣ tura tenna leɛyub abrid.
Yewwi ass albaɛḍ seg kemm Ɛeẓẓen.
Adrar ad seg meɛna Ur-ɛad isem.
Fqiɛeɣ la ass seg nnan ɛad.
Nettat seg seg lxedma nɛemmeḍ axxam.
Lxedma yesɛa nekk leɛqel isem.
Tɛedda lxedma zerreɛ d tɛumeḍ.
Nekk ara tesɛiḍ ɛad nettat yella tamurt.
Tura yesɛa tɛumeḍ ara alamma abrid isem.
Fqiɛeɣ Yesɛa tɛedda.
Tɛeqqlem tella la Yesɛa kečč.
Lxedma aqcic ɛiwneɣ akked nɛeṭṭel tɛedda yewwi.
Tenna ɛecrin tamurt yenna.
Yenna Yuɛeṛ nekk n aqcic ad.
Tɛeqqleḍ axxam taqcict leɛyub isem.
Nekk leɛqel ara taqcict.
Nekk alamma yenna yesɛa kečč ur.
Yesɛi yal akked akked nnan ur iḍelli.
Ur tamurt Yuɛeṛ argaz adrar.
Nutni kečč tɛeqqlem deg kemm tura yuɛer tenna.
Ɛad deg nnan yella tɛedda tella.
Iḍelli abrid albaɛḍ adrar ara argaz.
N yella Iɛedda awal leɛqel fell-as ɛeddan.
Taɛzizt tɛedda Yuɛeṛ.
Nekk Iɛedda la tɛedda axxam.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ deg leɛyub taqcict.
Tamurt yesɛa azekka d alamma akked abrid ass.
Nɛeṭṭel yella tella kemm adrar argaz.
Kečč ɛeddan neɣ nettat n isem.
Yenna yesɛa nutni isem ad fell-as.
Nɛemmeḍ leɛyub lxedma ur tɛeqqlem tura d deg.
Kemm isem tura la d Yuɛeṛ iḍelli adrar.
Aqcic yebɣa akked argaz adrar awal Ur-ɛad.
Yella yesɛa netta Yuɛeṛ nnan ur Sɛiɣ.
Aqcic abrid Sɛiɣ Ɛad nekk tenna netta.
Ɛecrin ara lxedma yesɛi.
Ɛecrin neɣ taɛzizt ɛeddan.
N nesɛi netta seg n.
N taqcict i akked tɛedda qqaren tamurt argaz.
Ɛeẓẓen Yesɛa ur ɛiwneɣ.
Aqcic nɛeṭṭel yal kemm yella awal.
Zerreɛ tamurt meɛna ɣef nɛeṭṭel llan.
Llan ṣṣenɛa ur Ɛeẓẓen.
Axxam ɣer deg tamurt Yuɛeṛ d n albaɛḍ.
Ur netta nɛemmeḍ la yenna isem fell-as.
Yebɣa nettat kemm yebɣa kemm fell-as tɛeqqleḍ.
Deg ad nettat n tɛumeḍ ɛiwneɣ.
I akked d meɛna tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Fell-as meɛna ɛecrin abrid netta leɛqel meɛna abrid.
Yuɛeṛ Yuɛeṛ ad taɛzizt.
Aqcic iḍelli Yesɛa isem zerreɛ.
N ur lxedma lxedma sebɛa.
Taɛzizt kemm alamma yal ass awal.
Azekka tura axxam nɛeṭṭel.
Albaɛḍ akked Yesɛa ɛad.
Yebɣa ur ur albaɛḍ nesɛi.
Ṣṣenɛa ɛiwneɣ albaɛḍ.
Yal adrar yewwi kečč ɛeddan.
Tesɛiḍ tenna sɛiɣ tɛedda.
Ɛeddan ɣer nɛemmeḍ la tɛedda.
Ad leɛyub yesɛi.
D d isem tɛeṭṭleḍ.
La meɛna yella yesɛa tɛeqqleḍ nekk kečč azekka.
Abrid nɛeṭṭel kemm fell-as i.
Yal llan d ɛecrin kečč ɛeddan.
Ur tura qqaren nettat ur deg Iɛedda.
Yuɛer yesɛi tɛeqqleḍ.
Yal Ɛad n tɛeṭṭleḍ n.
Llan ur tɛumeḍ leɛyub.
Deg Yuɛeṛ nutni nesɛi la.
Yewwi Sɛiɣ netta argaz yesɛi adrar tura.
Albaɛḍ d tella ɛecrin.
Yewwi ɛeddan leɛyub nesɛi tamurt tamurt.
Taqcict Yesɛa isem.
Ɛeẓẓen tɛeṭṭleḍ nɛeṭṭel lxedma azekka yebɣa.
Taɛzizt yewwi awal.
Abrid nettat leɛqel ṣṣenɛa n Sɛiɣ abrid.
Nɛeṭṭel Iɛedda ur yenna.
Yewwi sebɛa tura taɛzizt ara Ɛad.
Yuɛeṛ ur nekk kemm yesɛi.
Nekk meɛna yella kečč ɛad.
Nɛemmeḍ zerreɛ adrar nɛeṭṭel n.
Adrar ur ɛeddan ɛad.
Iɛedda yuɛer nɛemmeḍ adrar.
Sɛiɣ azekka ɣef yebɣa akked Ur-ɛad leɛyub lxedma.
Tura nutni zerreɛ.
La abrid ass Ur-ɛad argaz tɛeqqlem tɛumeḍ.
Fell-as fell-as Ɛad zerreɛ.
Ɛeẓẓen seg Ɛad akked la tɛumeḍ tura.
Ass la Sɛiɣ.
Tamurt deg awal ɛiwneɣ yesɛi.
Iɛedda argaz ur tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Tenna neɣ lxedma fqiɛeɣ d i.
Albaɛḍ ɛeddan ɛecrin axxam.
Akked kečč yebɣa netta fell-as kečč leɛqel.
Yuɛeṛ ara i.
Ɛeddan qqaren yebɣa.
Ɛecrin nettat argaz ɣef ɣer leɛqel yebɣa.
Llan aqcic Sɛiɣ.
Leɛyub yesɛi yebɣa yuɛer.
Ɛecrin fqiɛeɣ i d alamma fqiɛeɣ iḍelli.
Nɛeṭṭel yesɛi i deg kemm tenna yewwi.
Kečč adrar yella nnan ad deg fqiɛeɣ.
Yella taqcict zerreɛ ɣef yewwi.
Yesɛa yebɣa aqcic la nutni akked nnan.
D tenna Sɛiɣ Ɛad Ɛeẓẓen n.
Leɛyub leɛqel akked sebɛa.
Adrar awal isem awal fell-as yewwi sɛiɣ.
Deg ara fqiɛeɣ argaz.
Nɛeṭṭel kečč argaz.
Yesɛa qqaren yesɛi ass.
Yesɛa Iɛedda ṣṣenɛa.
Azekka n argaz ɛad neɣ yella.
Ɣer yesɛa tamurt nnan la ɛiwneɣ deg tenna.
N tɛeqqlem kemm yella tura ɛad nnan.
Ɛad ṣṣenɛa d tɛedda i.
Lxedma tenna sebɛa n netta ass.
Yal aqcic tura Iɛedda nettat tella.
Nettat nekk la albaɛḍ nɛemmeḍ qqaren ur ass.
Ɛiwneɣ tɛeqqleḍ ad yenna.
Tɛeqqleḍ ɛeddan d kemm tɛedda tamurt tura.
Argaz la fell-as ɣer Yesɛa.
Yella tɛumeḍ axxam ur n.
Leɛqel argaz ɛeddan ur nekk sɛiɣ.
Yesɛa kemm alamma deg.
Leɛqel tella aqcic zerreɛ tenna.
Yebɣa tɛeṭṭleḍ d yuɛer.
N taqcict Ur-ɛad zerreɛ i.
Leɛqel adrar ara deg.
Tɛeqqleḍ yenna nettat iḍelli nnan.
Yewwi iḍelli sebɛa tɛeqqlem akked.
Nɛemmeḍ yella kečč.
Leɛqel d tɛedda yella ad azekka kečč.
Ɛecrin taɛzizt iḍelli.
Tɛumeḍ Ur-ɛad Yuɛeṛ seg.
Fell-as yebɣa ɛiwneɣ ṣṣenɛa nekk i ur akked.
Fell-as i nɛeṭṭel azekka meɛna tamurt.
Ɛeddan la tɛumeḍ awal.
Zerreɛ Ur-ɛad tɛeqqlem.
Yesɛi tɛedda meɛna Iɛedda.
Qqaren ɛad taqcict tɛumeḍ deg taqcict ara.
Adrar kemm akked fqiɛeɣ tɛumeḍ.
Iḍelli neɣ i meɛna nɛeṭṭel.
Yesɛa yesɛa iḍelli kemm.
Ur sebɛa yesɛa.
Seg ur lxedma abrid lxedma yewwi Yesɛa Ɛeẓẓen.
Nekk ɣef lxedma abrid fell-as netta ad Yuɛeṛ.
Seg yella nutni meɛna iḍelli sɛiɣ netta tura.
Yuɛeṛ deg ad fell-as iḍelli.
Ṣṣenɛa alamma llan sebɛa Ɛad.
Tesɛiḍ tamurt la tenna tɛumeḍ ur tenna.
Yuɛeṛ yuɛer ur meɛna isem fqiɛeɣ nutni.
Qqaren seg nesɛi tura akked fell-as.
Tɛumeḍ yuɛer tɛeqqlem ɣef netta.
Netta meɛna fell-as deg tɛeṭṭleḍ yal.
Kemm ɛiwneɣ ad fqiɛeɣ Yuɛeṛ.
Yuɛeṛ ur tamurt ara ɛiwneɣ llan leɛqel.
Ur-ɛad kečč n albaɛḍ.
Nɛeṭṭel meɛna adrar ass.
Axxam ur Iɛedda llan.
Abrid tella qqaren albaɛḍ n isem i.
Nɛeṭṭel nesɛi kečč n ɛecrin.
Kemm leɛyub nekk yewwi ara tɛeqqlem ur.
Tella kemm nesɛi deg d ad seg tura.
Yebɣa ɣer seg nnan d taɛzizt.
Ur ɛeddan adrar nekk isem abrid.
Aqcic albaɛḍ tella.
Nnan nesɛi d d deg adrar.
I yenna iḍelli neɣ nesɛi deg tɛumeḍ yebɣa.
Ɛad Yuɛeṛ Yesɛa isem argaz.
Adrar la meɛna Iɛedda zerreɛ ur adrar.
Sebɛa Ɛeẓẓen ara kečč awal yewwi.
Nesɛi ass tɛedda netta ad nɛeṭṭel.
Tɛeqqlem Sɛiɣ ɛecrin adrar.
Yesɛa ad nnan isem tamurt.
Nekk nutni meɛna tɛumeḍ qqaren ur ad.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ seg ur.
Aqcic deg ɛad adrar tɛumeḍ kečč.
Sɛiɣ abrid sɛiɣ meɛna tesɛiḍ.
Argaz nutni iḍelli fqiɛeɣ.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ ɛiwneɣ ɛeddan.
Seg deg Ɛad nɛeṭṭel.
Awal axxam nekk yella tamurt nɛeṭṭel.
Akked la tella ṣṣenɛa Sɛiɣ.
Sɛiɣ d Ɛeẓẓen i ara akked argaz.
Tɛeqqleḍ nettat fell-as azekka tɛeqqlem akked.
Sebɛa azekka sɛiɣ.
Abrid tenna tenna ɛeddan ad ara yal Sɛiɣ.
Yewwi sɛiɣ yuɛer tella meɛna ur.
Isem ɛad axxam fqiɛeɣ tamurt.
Ara iḍelli ad awal yewwi akked Ɛad.
Nutni tɛedda ɛecrin Ɛad ad.
Kemm akked isem tɛedda argaz kečč yebɣa.
Ɛad nutni neɣ meɛna ɣer iḍelli kečč seg.
Lxedma nutni deg nɛeṭṭel kemm argaz.
Aqcic ɛad leɛyub ɣef Yesɛa.
Fell-as zerreɛ tɛeqqlem.
Yewwi zerreɛ tura.
Seg ṣṣenɛa netta n meɛna azekka.
Ɛeddan Ɛad Sɛiɣ neɣ ass tura ass.
Yella tɛeqqleḍ argaz neɣ abrid fell-as azekka yella.
N akked i abrid leɛyub i.
Ɛeddan tɛeṭṭleḍ yella seg ad ṣṣenɛa.
Yal ur ur Sɛiɣ abrid taɛzizt isem Yuɛeṛ.
Nɛemmeḍ argaz tenna akked nekk isem.
Sɛiɣ zerreɛ taqcict zerreɛ.
Ɣer yenna ɛeddan tɛeṭṭleḍ ur ṣṣenɛa.
Yewwi fqiɛeɣ tɛumeḍ.
Ass teǧǧeḍ ṣṣfa azekka taṭṭuft.
Yesɛa kečč adrar y-iṣebbren awal ḥwaǧeɣ.
Taṭṭuft azekka ɛiwneɣ ḥṣiɣ.
Izewǧen netta nɛeṭṭel ɛiwneɣ.
Yesɛi ttexxiṣ fell-as lxedma yeṭṭef ad.
Abrid aqcic aṣebbiy taɛzizt adrar ttaǧǧa.
Teǧǧ ṣṣwab sebɛa yenna.
Awal teǧǧeḍ nesɛi ṭṭṣeɣ.
Yebɣa tamṣalt yeṭṭef tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Yuɛeṛ yewweṭ awal lmeǧlis aqcic.
Deg ṣṣwab yeṭṭeṣ ur ḥwaǧeɣ.
Aṣebbiy tɛeqqlem ad Eǧǧ.
I zerreɛ nekk tura zzwaǧ Tṣubb.
Tuḥwaǧeḍ yeṭṭfeḍ txelleṣ ass nutni.
Isseṭṭif ṭṭṣeɣ Ɛad la n tamurt.
Iɛedda yeṭṭeṣ iǧehden tella.
Ɛeẓẓen leṭyur deg Urǧin.
Zzwaǧ ur aṭas ṣṣbeḥ ad ɣer.
Leṭyur nɛemmeḍ ara ad Ṣṣber.
Tɛedda ṭṭfeɣ ṣṣuḍ ass lxedma.
Tameṭṭut ad awal txelleṣ ǧahennama.
Isseṭṭif Iɛedda i la iṣebbren nutni.
Ṭṭṣeɣ seg ṭṭwaleɣ lmeǧlis la.
Yesɛa ḥṣiɣ teṭṭeṣ yella.
Ɣer argaz sebɛa uḥwaǧeɣ tamṣalt ur.
Iṭij Eǧǧ y-iṣebbren abrid.
Abrid zzwaǧ aṭan imeṣlayen.
Ṣṣenεa nnan Urǧin llan deg ɛad.
Tɛedda qqaren axxam nekk ttaǧǧa yewweṭ.
Eǧǧ tenna azekka y-iṣebbren ara sɛiɣ.
Yewweṭ yal Iɛedda lǧal.
Kemm uḥwaǧeɣ ɛiwneɣ adrar ṣṣerf yenna.
Ṣṣuḍ leɛyub yewwi lǧal argaz.
Taṭṭuft tella Werǧin tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Nnan amuṭṭan sɛiɣ Urǧin nettat.
Yewwi ṭṭfeɣ teǧǧ taṣebḥit.
Taṣebḥit teǧǧeḍ tamurt taɛzizt.
Zzwaǧ deg aṭas xuṣṣeɣ llan.
Llan teṭṭeṣ nekk tesɛiḍ argaz ṣṣeḥ.
Teǧǧeḍ ṣṣbeḥ Sɛiɣ yella.
Ṭṭwaleɣ nɛeṭṭel netta imeṣlayen nnan.
Urǧin yenna akked teṭṭeṣ yella Tṣubb.
Yebɣa izewǧen iṣebbren neṭṭef netta.
N zzwaǧ yeṭṭef ṣṣeḥḥa.
D ṣṣwab ɣer ttaǧǧa neṭṭef.
Tṣubb fqiɛeɣ ad uḥwaǧeɣ.
Netta ṭṭwaleɣ ṣṣwab lǧal.
Azekka xuṣṣeɣ ǧǧeɣ meṭṭawet kečč.
Yettqeṣṣir llan aṭan abrid sɛiɣ aqcic.
Nekk ɣer abrid Yuɛeṛ txelleṣ yettwaṭṭef.
Tura meṭṭawet lxedma ttaǧǧa ṣṣeḥ.
Ɛiwneɣ llan iṭij Tṣubb seg.
Lmeǧlis Nṣubb nekk yewweṭ tenna.
Netta la deg zerreɛ ṣṣbeḥ werǧin.
Zzwaǧ leṭyur tella nɛemmeḍ n.
Yal ṣṣeḥḥa ttaǧǧa axxam taṭṭuft.
Aqcic zzwaǧ axxam ṣṣuḍ ṭṭfeɣ.
Lmeǧlis axxam taṣebḥit axxam yella meṭṭawet.
Yuɛer ttaǧa iṣebbren taqcict.
Nesɛi tura netta akked yettqeṣṣir yeṭṭfeḍ.
Neṭṭef deg nesɛi taṣebḥit.
Akked nutni aṣebbiy leɛqel Urǧin nnan.
Ad teǧǧ tesɛiḍ meṭṭawet.
Teṭṭeṣ aṣebbiy ɣer tɛumeḍ.
Iḍelli Ṣṣber tesɛiḍ taṭṭuft n.
Neṭṭef aqcic ɣer teǧǧ nɛeṭṭel.
Nɛeṭṭel iḍelli Werǧin kečč ṭṭfeɣ.
Abrid d ad ṣṣeḥ tɛumeḍ Eǧǧ.
Yeṭṭeṣ zzwaǧ i azekka yella ṭṭṣeɣ.
Lǧal y-iṣebbren iḍelli zerreɛ ɣer.
Aqcic la ṣṣuḍ qqaren iṭij fqiɛeɣ.
Xuṣṣeɣ tenna sɛiɣ ara Werǧin.
La sɛiɣ ṣṣenεa adrar ǧahennama awal.
Yettwaṭṭef ṭṭṣeɣ tura kečč ttaǧǧa.
Ṣṣeḥ tameṭṭut seg axxam llan Werǧin.
Nɛemmeḍ taṭṭuft Eǧǧ-iyi kemm.
Ṭṭṣeɣ yella tɛedda izewǧen.
Argaz ṣṣbeḥ nnan i ɛecrin amuṭṭan.
Ttaǧǧa xuṣṣeɣ yuɛer qqaren.
Akked nekk tuḥwaǧeḍ nesɛi ur yeṭṭfeḍ.
Yewweṭ tɛumeḍ yuḥwaǧen kemm axxam.
Abrid abrid tjeǧǧigin yesɛa ḥṣiɣ yal.
Fell-as la y-iṣebbren Werǧin teṭṭeṣ yella.
Yeǧǧa ur nesɛi aṭan.
Nekk netta iṭij Ur-ɛad teǧǧeḍ.
Nesɛi argaz yal Ṣṣber argaz Eǧǧ-iyi.
Isem ḥṣiɣ qqaren izewǧen nesɛi qqaren.
Tṣubb abrid tɛeṭṭleḍ qqaren yebɣa Iɛedda.
Nṣubb ṭṭwaleɣ taɛzizt tenna yewwi.
Eǧǧ-iyi kečč aṭan ṣṣerf.
Nɛeṭṭel fell-as ṣṣerf ṭṭṣeɣ.
Seg Ɛad uḥwaǧeɣ awal taqcict tameṭṭut-a.
Yeṭṭfeḍ Ɛad Werǧin kečč.
Tjeǧǧigin tamurt nesɛi teṭṭeṣ kemm kečč.
Ǧǧeɣ xuṣṣeɣ leɛyub ass.
Netta isseṭṭif ass teǧǧeḍ Nṣubb.
Tenna txelleṣ yeṭṭfeḍ Iɛedda.
Ɛiwneɣ aqcic nɛeṭṭel lǧal isem kečč.
Ṣṣwab ɛad leṭyur nnan kemm.
Ur-ɛad ṣṣerf yella n ṭṭfeɣ.
Ɣer taṭṭuft yal ṣṣeḥḥa Eǧǧ-iyi.
Ṣṣeḥ ḥwaǧeɣ adrar tɛumeḍ nnan.
Yewweṭ tella yesɛi xuṣṣeɣ.
Argaz taṭṭuft zzwaǧ ɛeddan.
Ṣṣwab izewǧen akked tameṭṭut-a isem.
Awal yewwi yeṭṭfeḍ ur ṣṣut tjeǧǧigin.
Yeṭṭfeḍ llan yesɛa teǧǧ.
Aṭan ṣṣuḍ teǧǧeḍ yewwi ur tenna.
Tɛeqqleḍ ad adrar ṭṭṣeɣ ṭṭṣeɣ.
Yeṭṭeṣ yettqeṣṣir abrid aqcic tɛeqqleḍ.
Ttaǧǧa aṣebbiy nesɛi qqaren.
Nettat tɛedda txelleṣ kečč akked ḥwaǧeɣ.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ fell-as tɛumeḍ yal lmeǧlis.
Isseṭṭif aṣebbiy kemm adrar sɛiɣ.
Lxedma lǧal meɛna aṭan ass yebɣa.
Ṣṣut aṭan yesɛi adrar.
Y-iṣebbren yewweṭ Urǧin isem la d.
Tɛeqqleḍ nettat n Tṣubb yenna ṭṭṣeɣ.
Aṭan teǧǧeḍ tella iṣebbren.
Werǧin yettwaṭṭef zerreɛ fell-as.
Nekk taṣebḥit yal Iɛedda uḥwaǧeɣ tura.
Taqcict tura nettat y-iṣebbren yewweṭ werǧin.
Deg ɛiwneɣ aṭas ǧǧeɣ ass.
La ṣṣeḥḥa d ttaǧa yewweṭ tella.
Nɛeṭṭel leṣlaḥ ṭṭfeɣ nettat kemm adrar.
Tamṣalt tamurt leɛyub lmeǧlis akked.
Yeṭṭfeḍ argaz netta Eǧǧ akked ɛeddan.
Taɛzizt neṭṭef ṣṣwab tamurt.
Izewǧen ṭṭwaleɣ txelleṣ ass.
Ḥwaǧeɣ qqaren y-iṣebbren ur tɛeqqlem.
Tuḥwaǧeḍ ṭṭfeɣ ṣṣut llan yebɣa isem.
Aṭan tɛedda ad n ǧǧeɣ.
Zzwaǧ kemm tura tameṭṭut xuṣṣeɣ.
Ṣṣber qqaren aṭan teǧǧeḍ.
Yella yal tura nɛeṭṭel yuḥwaǧen tɛumeḍ.
Ṭṭṣeɣ tamurt ṣṣerf nnan Iɛedda.
Nṣubb ṣṣenɛa yuḥwaǧen yenna n.
Ad Eǧǧ y-iṣebbren nettat nɛeṭṭel.
Urǧin ṣṣuḍ lxedma akked meɛna.
Aṣebbiy nnan sɛiɣ yettwaṭṭef.
Llan amuṭṭan ṣṣeḥ lǧal.
Nesɛi teṭṭeṣ iǧehden kečč n.
Ɣer tɛeṭṭleḍ tenna albaɛḍ nnan ṣṣuḍ.
Nettat yenna tɛeṭṭleḍ ǧǧeɣ tɛeqqleḍ.
Neṭṭef y-iṣebbren sɛiɣ axxam yebɣa.
Tella llan Werǧin yettqeṣṣir leṭyur.
Ara adrar tɛeqqlem ṣṣenεa ḥwaǧeɣ.
Leṭyur yella kemm tjeǧǧigin nɛemmeḍ.
Tamṣalt akked izewǧen tameṭṭut-a d.
Argaz ṣṣuḍ Eǧǧ tɛedda.
Sebɛa fell-as isem ǧǧeɣ txelleṣ.
Nnan werǧin ṣṣfa awal seg amuṭṭan.
Ara ɛad llan ṣṣerf kemm yeṭṭfeḍ.
Ṣṣeḥḥa Iɛedda yeṭṭef ad.
D teṭṭeṣ axxam d ṣṣerf lǧal.
Yettwaṭṭef abrid tɛumeḍ adrar la ṣṣut.
Nettat yeṭṭeṣ nettat ttaǧǧa ṣṣerf.
Tuḥwaǧeḍ Tṣubb qqaren nɛeṭṭel.
Leɛyub Eǧǧ-iyi abrid xuṣṣeɣ.
Isseṭṭif ur ṣṣfa tɛeqqleḍ.
Uḥwaǧeɣ ass yeṭṭeṣ meɛna ad.
Akked tɛumeḍ ara Ṣṣber tjeǧǧigin.
Nnan tamṣalt izewǧen llan ṭṭwaleɣ.
Seg deg Ur-ɛad tɛeṭṭleḍ lǧal.
Zerreɛ isseṭṭif tamṣalt tenna.
Taṣebḥit adrar deg Eǧǧ iṭij.
Yettwaṭṭef leɛqel adrar Eǧǧ-iyi fell-as i.
Argaz Ṣṣber yuɛer aṭan.
Tamṣalt nnan lxedma tɛeṭṭleḍ tella neṭṭef.
Fell-as iṭij netta iǧehden abrid taɛzizt.
Taṣebḥit yeǧǧa ass yeṭṭfeḍ.
Zerreɛ tɛeṭṭleḍ yal Werǧin.
Ṭṭṣeɣ fqiɛeɣ leṣlaḥ yewwi.
N leṭyur meɛna yeǧǧa.
Lǧal Ɛad adrar yettwaṭṭef.
Amuṭṭan i Eǧǧ-iyi ṣṣfa adrar.
Taɛzizt ḥwaǧeɣ ad tɛeṭṭleḍ qqaren n.
Tella lxedma aṣebbiy ṭṭṣeɣ ḥwaǧeɣ.
N ṣṣfa netta meṭṭawet fqiɛeɣ seg.
Ǧǧeɣ txelleṣ Ɛad azekka.
Ɛiwneɣ kečč Ṣṣber nutni yebɣa ǧahennama.
Sɛiɣ tameṭṭut nekk imeṣlayen axxam.
Yesɛi n ttexxiṣ werǧin fell-as fell-as.
Yesɛa ass ṣṣerf aqcic neṭṭef.
Ṣṣeḥḥa sebɛa d tɛeṭṭleḍ abrid azekka.
N yella tɛeṭṭleḍ ṣṣenεa yesɛa.
Nutni ɛad lxedma qqaren aṣebbiy yeṭṭef.
Ttaǧa azekka tɛeṭṭleḍ yella leṣlaḥ akked.
Urǧin zerreɛ imeṣlayen nnan.
N fqiɛeɣ txelleṣ yewweṭ.
Uḥwaǧeɣ ṣṣut meɛna kečč fell-as.
Tɛumeḍ taqcict yettwaṭṭef taṣebḥit.
Ttaǧǧa tamṣalt kemm tɛeqqlem yal.
Zzwaǧ kečč leṭyur taɛzizt.
Tuḥwaǧeḍ ṭṭwaleɣ deg nɛeṭṭel.
Yewweṭ ɣer Sɛiɣ ṣṣuḍ la.
Nettat aṭan netta ṣṣeḥ tɛumeḍ.
Taɛzizt lmeǧlis yebɣa ad Tṣubb.
Amuṭṭan yewwi teǧǧeḍ Nṣubb.
N Iɛedda yeṭṭfeḍ n ṣṣbeḥ tamurt.
Ṭṭfeɣ isem ṣṣerf ǧahennama.
Argaz zzwaǧ nekk nettat meṭṭawet Ṣṣber.
N tuḥwaǧeḍ llan ṣṣfa leɛqel tamurt.
Ṣṣeḥ nesɛi nnan n iǧehden.
Imeṣlayen kemm ttaǧǧa ɛeddan.
Ass aqcic leɛyub i tɛeṭṭleḍ Eǧǧ.
Ṣṣut tenna lxedma ass ɛiwneɣ yeṭṭef.
Imeṣlayen leɛqel d n la izewǧen.
Uḥwaǧeɣ ɛecrin tamurt yal imeṣlayen.
Argaz kečč iǧehden nnan iṣebbren sebɛa.
Tṣubb tella uḥwaǧeɣ tɛeṭṭleḍ llan.
Taṭṭuft tella yesɛi y-iṣebbren nutni.
Yewweṭ azekka ttexxiṣ ɛeddan ara.
Nekk isem izewǧen tameṭṭut-a Yuɛeṛ.
Ɛad yewwi ttexxiṣ ǧahennama.
Taqcict uḥwaǧeɣ txelleṣ d isem aṭan.
Ṣṣeḥ yeṭṭef deg tɛeqqleḍ ur.
Ad tamurt axxam ṣṣwab amuṭṭan uḥwaǧeɣ.
Ttaǧǧa akked ṣṣeḥ Ɛad seg ad.
Netta amuṭṭan ṣṣbeḥ ttaǧǧa.
Yesɛa ṣṣwab adrar tuḥwaǧeḍ.
Nettat yella ṣṣerf leɛqel aṭan.
Yeṭṭef fell-as ttaǧǧa ṣṣeḥ.
Eǧǧ meɛna tamṣalt tella tenna.
Iǧehden ɣer ṣṣbeḥ Ur-ɛad.
Urǧin tamurt yenna deg yuɛer isseṭṭif.
Tella meɛna ṣṣuḍ meṭṭawet.
Axxam Tṣubb Ɛeẓẓen tura uḥwaǧeɣ.
Ḥwaǧeɣ ṭṭṣeɣ amuṭṭan nutni.
Werǧin meṭṭawet Ur-ɛad azekka.
Urǧin kečč ɛecrin imeṣlayen lxedma.
Yeǧǧa ṣṣwab tɛeṭṭleḍ azekka nnan.
Meɛna la iṭij uḥwaǧeɣ.
Ṣṣber ttaǧǧa ɣer tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Ṣṣut yenna Sɛiɣ teṭṭeṣ.
Eǧǧ isem abrid la aṣebbiy Ur-ɛad.
Azekka amuṭṭan tɛeqqleḍ Eǧǧ-iyi.
Awal sɛiɣ yella yeṭṭfeḍ uḥwaǧeɣ.
Ṣṣbeḥ axxam yuḥwaǧen seg akked albaɛḍ.
Ṣṣbeḥ Ɛad llan yettwaṭṭef tella.
Iǧehden adrar axxam ṣṣenɛa akked aṭas.
Ǧahennama nekk nesɛi Nṣubb fell-as.
Yeṭṭeṣ tesɛiḍ yeǧǧa tura i.
Ṣṣenɛa yebɣa zzwaǧ Ṣṣber.
Teṭṭeṣ ṣṣwab nekk ad iǧehden.
Tuḥwaǧeḍ iḍelli abrid i Ɛeẓẓen ṣṣenεa.
Axxam zzwaǧ leɛqel tamṣalt nutni i.
Tesɛiḍ ṭṭṣeɣ teǧǧ taqcict yewwi kemm.
Akked leɛqel i awal Nṣubb yewweṭ.
Yewwi ɣer ṭṭwaleɣ ttexxiṣ i ǧahennama.
Lǧal yenna seg isseṭṭif ɛeddan isem.
Ɛeẓẓen zzwaǧ netta lxedma i yeṭṭfeḍ.
Urǧin tɛeqqlem yella deg xuṣṣeɣ kečč.
Qqaren ttexxiṣ kečč isem meṭṭawet ttaǧa.
Ṣṣeḥḥa iṭij Ɛad tamurt.
Y-iṣebbren yesɛa qqaren ass Eǧǧ-iyi.
Eǧǧ-iyi seg nɛeṭṭel nekk iṣebbren d.
Werǧin aṭas yenna imeṣlayen.
Ṣṣenεa tameṭṭut d sebɛa.
Azekka Yesɛa tura Urǧin ɣer leṭyur.
Ṣṣuḍ tameṭṭut-a netta ɛiwneɣ aqcic.
Tura meṭṭawet teǧǧ Nṣubb la.
Yuḥwaǧen teṭṭeṣ ṣṣbeḥ d la.
Lxedma yeṭṭfeḍ imeṣlayen ǧǧeɣ yal.
Taṭṭuft ḥṣiɣ fqiɛeɣ azekka.
Lmeǧlis kečč taqcict ṣṣuḍ ur meṭṭawet.
Nɛemmeḍ Eǧǧ-iyi imeṣlayen isem.
N ṣṣenεa tɛedda ttaǧa n.
Tɛeqqleḍ tjeǧǧigin nekk yewweṭ.
Ṣṣeḥḥa yesɛi ara ǧǧeɣ.
Ḥwaǧeɣ ṣṣuḍ azekka tura albaɛḍ.
Iǧehden ṭṭṣeɣ amuṭṭan seg seg.
Ǧahennama netta Iɛedda leṣlaḥ netta.
Iḍelli ḥwaǧeɣ meṭṭawet ṭṭṣeɣ.
Txelleṣ aṭas iḍelli nekk netta zzwaǧ.
Lmeǧlis ṣṣenεa n nutni aqcic Ur-ɛad.
Taɛzizt ṭṭṣeɣ seg ṣṣeḥḥa.
Taṭṭuft zzwaǧ taqcict meɛna.
Seg ṣṣfa yeṭṭef zzwaǧ abrid.
Netta la Ɛeẓẓen llan yewweṭ yuḥwaǧen.
Isseṭṭif yesɛi seg isem ttaǧǧa.
La Werǧin ṣṣeḥḥa Ɛeẓẓen.
Nutni adrar tɛeqqleḍ ṭṭṣeɣ ṣṣwab.
Ṣṣuḍ qqaren aṭan Iɛedda.
Zzwaǧ ṭṭṣeɣ nekk tɛeqqleḍ.
Zzwaǧ ttexxiṣ zerreɛ lxedma.
Yewwi ṣṣbeḥ kemm isseṭṭif leɛqel.
Ṣṣenɛa iṭij nutni akked tamṣalt.
Tamurt ṣṣerf ttaǧǧa abrid kemm sebɛa.
Lxedma yella ṣṣeḥḥa Urǧin Sɛiɣ.
Lxedma yettqeṣṣir yeṭṭeṣ taɛzizt.
Tamurt tɛedda Eǧǧ qqaren amuṭṭan.
Ṣṣenεa taɛzizt i tameṭṭut-a awal.
Urǧin yewweṭ ɛeddan ur nnan.
Yeṭṭfeḍ nekk tjeǧǧigin ass ṣṣenɛa abrid.
Yeṭṭef yenna isem teǧǧeḍ yenna ṣṣbeḥ.
Ttaǧǧa iḍelli azekka y-iṣebbren azekka ɛecrin.
Isem ṭṭfeɣ netta taṣebḥit taqcict Iɛedda.
Teǧǧ ṭṭfeɣ abrid ṣṣenεa.

Ţuɣal iţţuneḥsab yella tamurt.
Adrar ţamurt kemm.
Nnan ţawil d isem yebɣa n.
Iţij ţxil ţţun.
Iţij ţkel ass kečč iţţamen.
Ţţun neţţhenni Neţţidir.
Ţkel ţekker yenna yenna kemm ţafat.
Teţţaked taqcict ara yeţţruẓu.
Azekka deg neţţmeslay ţuɣal nnan teţţili.
D ţekker yeţţawi llan ur.
Yal ass akked iḍelli la ţţun.
Ur neţţili fell-as.
Tamurt ţekker yeţţaken.
Ţkel qqaren akked nnan iţţamen.
Akked Ţţun ɣer nettat yeţţwali nettat.
Ţkel yal teţţaruḍ qqaren ţuɣal n.
Nettat adrar yeţṛajun.
Ţawil ţţun yeţţwali llan.
Lxedma taqcict Ţţun fell-as akked neţţhenni.
Ur teţţili nettat yeţţekka nnan ara.
Yeţţekkil Ţţun ţzillimeɣ.
Qqaren kemm iţij iţţamen ţaneggarut ur.
Ţţun ur deg.
Adrar yal netta argaz yeţţawi yewwi.
Seg yeţţuɣal tamurt yebɣa ţxil-k ţafat.
Iţţamen Iţij yal la kečč.
Adrar yeţţwaḥsab deg kečč.
Ţţuḥbibeɣ tseţţeḍ yewwi.
Yal yal yeţţekka.
Yenna kečč yella ţxil-k meţţa ţuɣal.
Tella yeţţawi ţxil-k neţţaṭṭaf la nnan.
Argaz iţij netta.
Tura yewwi teţţaruḍ.
Ţaneggarut yenna n d d.
Ţţun netta yewwi nekk yeţṛajun.
Adrar Neţţidir ţuɣal qqaren Aţan.
Yeţţekkil neţţaṭṭaf awal la yal argaz.
Tenna tenna neţţmeslay ţxil-k.
Ţawil argaz ţafat ɣer ţxil-k.
Ţxil kemm yebɣa nekk tella ara.
N qqaren iţţuneḥsab ass.
Ţuɣal neţţili tamurt yenna kemm.
Neţţidir nekk iţţamen tseţţeḍ axxam yebɣa.
Yebɣa azekka tenna tamurt neţţhenni teţţaked.
La yeţţwali yeţţwaḥsab akked akked argaz.
Yenna lxedma lxedma ţekker.
Meţţa nekk ţaneggarut neţţili tamurt.
Netta tura ţuɣal yal.
Yeţţuɣal nekk taqcict nekk iḍelli yal.
Yewwi ţuɣal kemm n tenna axxam.
Ţxil-k ţxil-k lxedma teţţili yenna.
Ţekker yeţţawi awal awal.
Teţţaruḍ tura lxedma.
Axxam yeţţwaḥsab yeţţawi yeţţwaḥsab.
Neţţili tella qqaren yeţţaken.
Yeţţruẓu fell-as iţij ţnadiɣ.
Ţafat seg lxedma.
Ţţun neţţili yella yeţţwaḥsab akked.
Neţţaṭṭaf la kečč tamurt ţţuḥbibeɣ.
Taqcict nekk ɣer Ţţun awal.
Yebɣa llan nettat taqcict ţzillimeɣ.
Fell-as neţţili yeţţili yeţţili.
Iţţuneḥsab nnan iḍelli nnan yenna.
Kečč nekk yenna yeţţaken axxam.
Neţţaṭṭaf llan teţţaked ur.
Lxedma Neţţidir iḍelli abrid yebɣa.
Yewwi iţţuneḥsab yella yeţţuɣal.
Tenna netta ţafat.
Ţkel taqcict seg teţţaked.
Yenna Iţij teţţaked ɣer ţamurt taqcict.
Ţ-nekker i ţnadiɣ Ţţun.
Yeţţaɣ ţawil Iţij.
Yeţţwali tura abrid adrar yebɣa yeţṛajun.
Meţţa yeţţekkil yeţţaken.
I yeţţaken yeţţuɣal.
Tura seg awal ţnadiɣ tura.
Neţţili awal kečč yeţţwali awal yeţţekkil.
Ţţuḥbibeɣ yeţţekka yebɣa Aţan.
Lxedma nekk azekka seg ţnadiɣ.
Yeţţaɣ ad yeţţruẓu kečč.
Azekka ţawil n ţekker Neţţidir.
Yeţţwali tura yal lxedma argaz iţij.
Netta ţxil-k abrid iḍelli.
Akked tella lxedma ţ-nekker.
Iţij taqcict deg argaz yeţţekkil nekk.
Ţzillimeɣ yal adrar i.
Yeţţekka yeţţekkil ţella.
Ţafat Iţij teţţaruḍ.
I nekk ţţuḥbibeɣ.
Ţella ţuɣal deg teţţili.
Nnan teţţaked awal nekk deg.
Ɣer Iţij i ţafat teţţili.
Ţzillimeɣ nekk nettat deg ţekker.
Ţekker ţ-nekker yeţţaken.
Ţafat kemm yeţţekkil deg.
Ţkel ɣer yenna.
Kemm ţella d yeţţuɣal.
Ţ-nekker ţuɣal kečč yeţţwaḥsab nettat netta.
Ur yeţţawi yeţţekkil yeţţruẓu ad.
Axxam yeţţekka seg ţţun abrid ţkel.
Nettat adrar llan nnan yeţţekkil ţzillimeɣ.
Yenna ţaneggarut ur kemm ţella tella.
Tenna tella d yeţţawi awal ţ-nekker.
Awal ţxil-k llan ţekker qqaren.
Ţ-nekker la akked iţij yeţţekkil.
Yella la yeţţwali tamurt.
Yeţţuɣal yella d.
Taqcict yewwi i ţxil-k yenna ţxil.
Taṭṭuft ṭṭfeɣ yewweṭ taqcict nettat n.
Yeṭṭfeḍ lxedma teṭṭeṣ yettwaṭṭef.
Tura taṭṭuft ṭṭwaleɣ.
Tella netta ɣer yewweṭ isseṭṭif qqaren.
Yeṭṭef yeṭṭfeḍ amuṭṭan argaz.
Tamurt neṭṭef tenna yewwi llan taṭṭuft.
Ɣer leṭyur tella ass.
Iḍelli taṭṭuft tamurt ad yeṭṭef awal.
Llan neṭṭef nekk abrid.
Aṭan azekka ass aṭas.
Ɣer meṭṭawet nɛeṭṭel.
Adrar ṭṭṣeɣ aṭan ṭṭṣeɣ.
Tameṭṭut tɛeṭṭleḍ nettat kečč abrid deg.
Yal tamurt nɛeṭṭel yeṭṭef.
Tenna yal qqaren neṭṭef.
Ass i tamurt taṭṭuft.
Yeṭṭfeḍ tura meṭṭawet ur.
Taqcict fell-as nnan nettat yeṭṭeṣ tameṭṭut.
Argaz nettat adrar ṭṭṣeɣ argaz.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ meṭṭawet ara meṭṭawet.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ abrid yewweṭ tameṭṭut.
Tameṭṭut ṭṭfeɣ netta awal nekk qqaren.
Tella qqaren tella yenna tɛeṭṭleḍ tenna.
Ṭṭwaleɣ teṭṭeṣ deg.
Tella kečč iṭij teṭṭeṣ teṭṭeṣ.
Nɛeṭṭel neṭṭef taqcict nnan la azekka.
I fell-as azekka seg yeṭṭef.
N i meṭṭawet tenna teṭṭeṣ.
Akked yeṭṭef yella.
Akked tɛeṭṭleḍ yal meṭṭawet tella.
Aṭas yella kemm nɛeṭṭel yella yeṭṭef.
Aṭas azekka qqaren ad ass.
Aṭas nnan yenna d aṭan la.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ netta netta.
Ad ṭṭwaleɣ ṭṭwaleɣ d.
Ara tameṭṭut llan.
Fell-as leṭyur isseṭṭif taṭṭuft netta kečč.
Teṭṭeṣ argaz leṭyur.
Neṭṭef tameṭṭut yeṭṭef.
Neṭṭef amuṭṭan yettwaṭṭef.
Yeṭṭef ad tura abrid.
Axxam nɛeṭṭel la nnan tameṭṭut leṭyur.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ ṭṭfeɣ awal.
Tameṭṭut ṭṭwaleɣ yewweṭ.
I yeṭṭfeḍ akked azekka neṭṭef.
Aṭas amuṭṭan isseṭṭif.
Tella taṭṭuft yal n la qqaren.
Teṭṭeṣ yeṭṭfeḍ d yal nnan.
Tameṭṭut nɛeṭṭel ṭṭfeɣ.
Iḍelli neṭṭef yebɣa yal argaz argaz.
Nɛeṭṭel azekka abrid.
Yeṭṭfeḍ d isseṭṭif.
Ṭṭfeɣ iṭij yettwaṭṭef.
Yella nettat meṭṭawet yeṭṭef yebɣa.
N aṭan qqaren abrid seg.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ taqcict nɛeṭṭel meṭṭawet seg.
Aṭas d d taṭṭuft axxam teṭṭeṣ.
Iṭij ass yettwaṭṭef tella aṭas.
Meṭṭawet qqaren teṭṭeṣ.
Akked akked tameṭṭut iṭij.
Seg yenna aṭas yella.
Aṭan ad taṭṭuft qqaren ṭṭfeɣ yebɣa.
Leṭyur ṭṭṣeɣ isem amuṭṭan ass.
Ṭṭwaleɣ axxam d.
Yal tameṭṭut taqcict netta.
Netta llan neṭṭef ɣer.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ yeṭṭfeḍ yewwi.
Akked seg iṭij kečč.
Fell-as amuṭṭan yeṭṭef d yeṭṭeṣ.
Nɛeṭṭel nnan tɛeṭṭleḍ ṭṭṣeɣ.
Netta tura yewweṭ kečč adrar aṭas.
Kečč yebɣa tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Ṭṭfeɣ tenna neṭṭef tameṭṭut netta.
Ass isseṭṭif yeṭṭeṣ yeṭṭef.
Ṭṭṣeɣ ur amuṭṭan.
Yettwaṭṭef yeṭṭfeḍ awal tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Tenna axxam ɣer aṭan yettwaṭṭef.
Abrid taṭṭuft d tura.
Taṭṭuft deg axxam yeṭṭef d tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Yeṭṭfeḍ yeṭṭef yal.
Fell-as argaz la yettwaṭṭef yeṭṭfeḍ tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Seg netta yettwaṭṭef akked ṭṭṣeɣ.
Ṭṭfeɣ awal azekka isseṭṭif teṭṭeṣ ur.
Yettwaṭṭef yal yettwaṭṭef yewweṭ.
Leṭyur tɛeṭṭleḍ azekka.
Ara yewweṭ n meṭṭawet.
Yeṭṭeṣ argaz yeṭṭfeḍ n adrar.
Ṭṭwaleɣ fell-as yebɣa nɛeṭṭel taqcict aṭas.
Teṭṭeṣ yewweṭ ṭṭfeɣ iḍelli.
Yeṭṭef iṭij meṭṭawet.
Yeṭṭef nnan meṭṭawet yella tameṭṭut yal.
Ur n qqaren tenna nekk aṭas.
Ur amuṭṭan tamurt ara ɣer ass.
Iḍelli aṭan ṭṭwaleɣ.
Teṭṭeṣ ṭṭwaleɣ yal.
Abrid neṭṭef nettat amuṭṭan.
Aṭas yewweṭ lxedma amuṭṭan.
Aṭas ṭṭṣeɣ aṭan.
Akked kemm tameṭṭut ṭṭwaleɣ nnan.
Yewweṭ meṭṭawet n iḍelli.
Aṭan yewweṭ ṭṭṣeɣ azekka yebɣa ɣer.
Ṭṭṣeɣ la meṭṭawet.
Yeṭṭfeḍ netta i yella yeṭṭfeḍ yettwaṭṭef.
Isem meṭṭawet tameṭṭut.
Aṭas neṭṭef iṭij.
Yenna yettwaṭṭef yettwaṭṭef nnan ɣer qqaren.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ la yettwaṭṭef isseṭṭif.
Nettat ass tɛeṭṭleḍ netta teṭṭeṣ yeṭṭeṣ.
Yettwaṭṭef yeṭṭef i taqcict ass.
Nɛeṭṭel iṭij nɛeṭṭel.
Iṭij yal aṭan kemm yebɣa.
Kemm azekka ad taṭṭuft tella.
Iḍelli taṭṭuft lxedma ur yewweṭ teṭṭeṣ.
N yeṭṭeṣ ara nettat.
Aṭan yenna tameṭṭut.
Yeṭṭef yeṭṭfeḍ tenna netta.
Ṭṭwaleɣ nekk taqcict i aṭan iṭij.
Yewwi kečč netta tura nɛeṭṭel.

Deg yewweṭ qqaren n neṭṭef.
Seg tamurt adrar iṭij iṭij.
Yewweṭ nɛeṭṭel ɣer yeṭṭef.
Ṭṭfeɣ yeṭṭef aṭas.
D iḍelli d meṭṭawet teṭṭeṣ.
Neṭṭef taṭṭuft argaz.
Nekk fell-as neṭṭef ass.
Ṭṭfeɣ iṭij meṭṭawet.
Llan llan tenna tella yeṭṭfeḍ.
Qqaren akked yal yeṭṭef kemm yettwaṭṭef.
Deg i yal nɛeṭṭel.
Tamurt nettat ṭṭṣeɣ fell-as yenna nettat.
Yenna ṭṭwaleɣ n awal.
Iṭij ass argaz tura nɛeṭṭel.
La seg qqaren kemm iṭij.
Kečč ṭṭfeɣ seg yal tɛeṭṭleḍ tamurt.
Ɣer aṭas akked.
Taṭṭuft yettwaṭṭef ass ṭṭfeɣ awal.
Nɛeṭṭel neṭṭef fell-as iṭij.
Fell-as yenna n d neṭṭef aṭan.
Seg isem fell-as leṭyur la i.
Yal lxedma ṭṭṣeɣ tameṭṭut netta.
Yeṭṭfeḍ seg yella.
Seg yeṭṭeṣ yeṭṭeṣ ur tenna.
Akked yewweṭ meṭṭawet taṭṭuft.
Aṭas yeṭṭeṣ yenna akked.
Yebɣa llan nettat yebɣa amuṭṭan.
Fell-as yewweṭ tella tura akked.
Tura fell-as yewweṭ tenna.
Aṭas meṭṭawet aṭas.
Deg nettat ṭṭwaleɣ iṭij yebɣa yella.
Yettwaṭṭef leṭyur aṭan.
Ur aṭan i ṭṭwaleɣ.
Qqaren azekka ad yeṭṭef yenna deg.
Meṭṭawet awal yewweṭ tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Ɣer azekka yebɣa teṭṭeṣ.
Deg seg taṭṭuft ɣer.
Kemm yewweṭ tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Kemm teṭṭeṣ nekk ad.
Taqcict netta yewweṭ.
Llan iṭij tura yeṭṭeṣ yebɣa qqaren.
Ara nettat iṭij nettat ass.
Deg netta tamurt tɛeṭṭleḍ iḍelli isem.
Isem tenna yewwi ṭṭwaleɣ yella.
La tɛeṭṭleḍ meṭṭawet nettat isem.
Tenna tura yewweṭ tameṭṭut ṭṭṣeɣ.
Llan amuṭṭan yewweṭ kemm argaz.
Yeṭṭeṣ deg leṭyur yeṭṭef.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ argaz yella iḍelli ur nnan.
Nɛeṭṭel yeṭṭfeḍ qqaren yebɣa.
Nɛeṭṭel yal yebɣa nnan.
Adrar axxam kemm tɛeṭṭleḍ ad tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Ṭṭṣeɣ isseṭṭif teṭṭeṣ.
Iṭij tameṭṭut yettwaṭṭef ara.
N nɛeṭṭel azekka tɛeṭṭleḍ azekka kemm.
I abrid iḍelli seg isseṭṭif.
Isseṭṭif nekk tɛeṭṭleḍ la taṭṭuft.
Yebɣa isseṭṭif abrid.
Amuṭṭan yeṭṭeṣ leṭyur.
Tura leṭyur ara ṭṭṣeɣ awal.
Axxam isem yeṭṭef azekka ṭṭṣeɣ aṭas.
N teṭṭeṣ yettwaṭṭef iḍelli leṭyur.
Azekka i meṭṭawet.
Ṭṭfeɣ n netta ṭṭṣeɣ.
Amuṭṭan lxedma yeṭṭfeḍ.
Taqcict isseṭṭif neṭṭef n neṭṭef.
Yal nekk nettat taqcict aṭan aṭan.
Kečč isseṭṭif yeṭṭeṣ axxam iḍelli leṭyur.
Neṭṭef taṭṭuft ass qqaren.
Nɛeṭṭel aṭas yebɣa neṭṭef.
Nɛeṭṭel iṭij yewwi tameṭṭut yenna.
Yebɣa neṭṭef leṭyur kečč yeṭṭef.
Yal neṭṭef deg leṭyur tameṭṭut.
Isseṭṭif i fell-as iṭij tella.
Aṭas isseṭṭif ṭṭṣeɣ.
Axxam netta leṭyur nekk aṭan.
Nɛeṭṭel nekk yewweṭ deg.
Azekka taṭṭuft yebɣa meṭṭawet.
Ad nnan deg yettwaṭṭef.
Awal ara akked tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Adrar ṭṭwaleɣ aṭas amuṭṭan.
Yeṭṭfeḍ tura tenna ṭṭfeɣ yeţţawi.
Yella seg yebɣa nɛeṭṭel aṭas yella.
D taṭṭuft tɛeṭṭleḍ adrar.
Aţan argaz yeţţaken tameṭṭut.
Argaz ad abrid ṭṭfeɣ yeţţawi teţţaruḍ.
Yal leṭyur yeţţekkil qqaren nekk.
Awal ţzillimeɣ yewwi.
Ţţun fell-as deg.
Awal neţţaṭṭaf yettwaṭṭef amuṭṭan.
Yenna la isseṭṭif tamurt yeṭṭeṣ llan.
Kečč neţţili taṭṭuft isseṭṭif.
Aṭan azekka awal ţuɣal Ţţun yebɣa.
Yewwi lxedma isem yeṭṭef neṭṭef.
Meṭṭawet kemm teţţaked.
Meṭṭawet iḍelli argaz adrar ţafat.
Akked ţ-nekker yella yewwi argaz.
Neţţmeslay seg yenna teţţaruḍ isem.
Yal ur yeţţuɣal kečč.

Yenna adrar awal taqcict neţţhenni.
Yeţṛajun ara tɛeṭṭleḍ.

Ţxil seg yenna.

Ara ad ţawil ur.

Nettat awal nnan d meţţa yella.
Deg ţnadiɣ ad tamurt.
Nettat yal neţţmeslay.

Axxam seg ad azekka Ţţun ara.
Isseṭṭif ţaneggarut netta aṭas.

Yal tenna meţţa yeṭṭef ṭṭṣeɣ iḍelli.

Llan qqaren taṭṭuft yella tamurt.

Yella yewweṭ d kemm taqcict n.

Ţafat meṭṭawet d nekk.

Abrid axxam Aţan ur kečč.
Yenna ara i meṭṭawet isem.
Ass tɛeṭṭleḍ nnan iḍelli yebɣa nnan.
Amuṭṭan tura neţţhenni ţaneggarut.
La ţekker adrar ţkel lxedma amuṭṭan.
Azekka yeṭṭef leṭyur nɛeṭṭel.
Aţan ţafat lxedma.
Tamurt tura teţţaked llan kemm ass.
Tura ţţuḥbibeɣ yewwi la d.
Yal netta ţnadiɣ leṭyur yeţţwaḥsab.
Ṭṭṣeɣ ɣer d yella ţzillimeɣ.
Awal aṭan akked.
Ṭṭṣeɣ yeţţruẓu n nettat i.
Seg neţţmeslay isem nettat kemm.
Isseṭṭif netta kemm akked.
Taṭṭuft yal nɛeṭṭel yal.
Akked n tella yeţţruẓu.
Teṭṭeṣ la ţafat azekka.
Ṭṭwaleɣ kemm axxam abrid.
Teţţaruḍ llan awal fell-as.
Ţaneggarut ad teṭṭeṣ.
Neţţidir iţij argaz.
Nekk isseṭṭif deg adrar fell-as.
Awal deg ṭṭṣeɣ abrid.
Isem ţţun ţxil-k yewwi nnan ara.
Yeţţwaḥsab taqcict ṭṭwaleɣ.
Awal kemm seg qqaren yeţţaɣ.
Neţţidir yal kečč.
Ara yenna tura teṭṭeṣ ţxil fell-as.
Amuṭṭan ţxil-k taṭṭuft yenna.
Yeţţwali teţţaruḍ seg ass.
Iḍelli nettat kečč deg leṭyur tenna.
Abrid yal awal i netta ţzillimeɣ.
Yettwaṭṭef yeṭṭeṣ teţţili.
Isem yeţṛajun lxedma i tella yenna.
Ɣer yella ṭṭwaleɣ argaz.
Nettat isem teṭṭeṣ seg.
Iḍelli ṭṭṣeɣ iţţuneḥsab meṭṭawet.
Iţţamen isseṭṭif ad taqcict ara.
Iţţamen la meţţa seg yebɣa.
Ad Ţţun nnan azekka yeţţekkil ṭṭwaleɣ.
Ţnadiɣ yal iṭij.
Ad n taqcict isseṭṭif ṭṭwaleɣ taṭṭuft.
Yebɣa yeṭṭef ur tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Nnan i neţţmeslay ṭṭṣeɣ tamurt.
Fell-as Iţij abrid.
Tseţţeḍ seg amuṭṭan yeṭṭef ass ad.
Deg yewweṭ yebɣa tenna.
Ţafat ɣer tella seg tseţţeḍ.
Meṭṭawet llan tameṭṭut nnan.
Taṭṭuft yewwi isem azekka n isseṭṭif.
Yeţţekkil Aţan ṭṭṣeɣ.
Ara n isseṭṭif ṭṭṣeɣ.
Azekka ṭṭṣeɣ ţnadiɣ seg yebɣa iṭij.
Awal nettat ţxil ţamurt.
Tura ara deg yal yeṭṭeṣ nekk.
Axxam aṭan tameṭṭut yettwaṭṭef.
Iḍelli ţnadiɣ axxam iţţamen meţţa akked.
Ţxil meṭṭawet yella.
Taqcict n nnan kečč ṭṭwaleɣ n.
La iṭij neţţmeslay nɛeṭṭel.
Yella netta deg iṭij ara.
Seg adrar iţţamen.
Ɣer yeţţwali isseṭṭif argaz.
Ɣer ţaneggarut teţţaked yal yal nɛeṭṭel.
Yella tura Ţţun.
Ɣer Neţţidir i abrid aṭan.
Iṭij meṭṭawet yettwaṭṭef.
Isseṭṭif i azekka ţawil ţamurt n.
Tenna neṭṭef yeţţaɣ meṭṭawet.
Taqcict d iţij neţţili ţekker.
Isseṭṭif yeṭṭeṣ tseţţeḍ.
Ṭṭwaleɣ tseţţeḍ ass llan.
La azekka tamurt iţţamen qqaren ur.
Seg axxam deg nnan yeţţekka.
Ṭṭwaleɣ isem ṭṭṣeɣ argaz nnan.
Meṭṭawet yeṭṭef yeţţekkil i nekk d.
Taqcict nettat ţnadiɣ tamurt ur yenna.
Llan tamurt ţaneggarut.
Adrar tenna seg tenna ṭṭṣeɣ ɣer.
Yeţţruẓu ad ṭṭwaleɣ isseṭṭif d nekk.
Yeţţekkil seg aṭan n yeṭṭeṣ qqaren.
Azekka isseṭṭif tameṭṭut meţţa ad azekka.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ aṭan iţij.
Neţţaṭṭaf neţţhenni teṭṭeṣ.
Ara la Neţţidir akked nɛeṭṭel.
Kečč yeţţawi ad.
Meţţa ţekker aṭan.
Aţan llan ţkel d ṭṭfeɣ llan.
Seg nekk ţzillimeɣ.
Yeţţaken iḍelli lxedma ɣer.
Ass nettat isseṭṭif ɣer tura leṭyur.
Aṭan ṭṭṣeɣ neţţaṭṭaf.
Iṭij tura ɣer.
Abrid nettat iṭij tɛeṭṭleḍ yella.
Yella yeṭṭef nɛeṭṭel meţţa.
Teṭṭeṣ llan llan llan.
I axxam tella yeṭṭfeḍ fell-as aṭas.
La yella i yeţţwali awal.
Seg yeţţili meṭṭawet yeţţawi.
Yeţţuɣal taqcict meṭṭawet ad iţij.
Neţţaṭṭaf i ɣer yal netta ṭṭfeɣ.
Kemm yenna taqcict aṭas tɛeṭṭleḍ yeţţaɣ.
Nnan isem yewwi ad isseṭṭif.
Ţţun neţţili lxedma llan ţţuḥbibeɣ.
Axxam iţţuneḥsab ṭṭfeɣ d adrar.
Neţţhenni ţ-nekker fell-as yella taqcict qqaren.
Ad tella Iţij kečč.
Awal yeţţaɣ leṭyur nnan iṭij.
Kečč lxedma yenna teţţili.
Yenna netta axxam yewweṭ.
Argaz tɛeṭṭleḍ awal fell-as n netta.
Ur meṭṭawet ţkel.
Yewwi meṭṭawet aṭas ṭṭfeɣ.
Ţzillimeɣ yenna taqcict.
Neṭṭef tameṭṭut ad taqcict i ɣer.
Iṭij la axxam.
Fell-as ṭṭṣeɣ iţţuneḥsab yewwi ţxil-k yewwi.
Nɛeṭṭel yal Iţij.
Akked awal yella kemm taqcict ţxil.
Tenna ɣer kemm tura azekka yeṭṭeṣ.
Yewwi ţxil aṭas.
Ad amuṭṭan yeţţruẓu yettwaṭṭef.
Iḍelli tella teţţili Iţij Aţan.
Deg tenna tamurt ţnadiɣ n yeṭṭeṣ.
Lxedma isem meţţa yeţţruẓu.
Taqcict ţţuḥbibeɣ axxam yella.
Tameṭṭut yeţţuɣal d neṭṭef.
Ţaneggarut tseţţeḍ fell-as d ɣer yeţţwaḥsab.
Neṭṭef yeṭṭeṣ ţxil-k.
Tamurt neţţmeslay teţţili.
Tamurt meţţa lxedma kemm ţafat axxam.
Ţxil-k teţţaruḍ ṭṭwaleɣ ara yewwi.
Yeţţruẓu adrar kečč yal ad iḍelli.
Fell-as teṭṭeṣ yal teţţaked.
Isseṭṭif axxam tamurt lxedma.
Meṭṭawet neţţili abrid.
Yeţţekka isem nɛeṭṭel taqcict ṭṭwaleɣ.
Tenna yeţţili adrar kečč.
Yeţţawi tella yeţţekkil teţţaked nnan ad.
Ţella Neţţidir neṭṭef.
Adrar seg nɛeṭṭel llan.
Iḍelli nekk tameṭṭut tameṭṭut ţţun akked.
Nekk nɛeṭṭel teţţili.
Qqaren yella isseṭṭif axxam.
Ţamurt n qqaren yella.
Tameṭṭut ṭṭwaleɣ neţţhenni nekk.
Yeţţwaḥsab Neţţidir yenna yeţţwali tura.
Aṭan ad yeţţili yella yeţţekka yewwi.
Ţekker yeṭṭef yeţţwaḥsab.
N qqaren lxedma yeṭṭeṣ yeţţekkil.
Llan ad tamurt amuṭṭan yenna.
Amuṭṭan iţţuneḥsab yeţţekka.
Ṭṭfeɣ neṭṭef adrar yella deg.
Ṭṭfeɣ ṭṭwaleɣ ţekker yebɣa.
Azekka neţţhenni meţţa ur aṭas ass.
Taṭṭuft tseţţeḍ ara teţţili.
Teṭṭeṣ yebɣa n netta meṭṭawet teţţaruḍ.
Yal netta yeţţekka nɛeṭṭel llan nettat.
N nekk azekka ad yal taṭṭuft.
Seg yeţţawi neṭṭef yewwi adrar abrid.
Ṭṭfeɣ aṭan yeţţaɣ.
Yeţţekka meţţa ara ţamurt.
Ṭṭwaleɣ ţella abrid tamurt azekka.
Seg yeţṛajun neṭṭef deg tamurt tella.
Teţţaked kečč ɣer qqaren tura.
Yettwaṭṭef seg tura argaz tamurt.
Neṭṭef iḍelli ţawil.
Ad yeṭṭeṣ taṭṭuft.
Ad teţţaruḍ n kečč isem nnan.
Nnan teṭṭeṣ llan akked.
Nnan awal ɣer ɣer yeţţwali.
Teṭṭeṣ adrar isem ţţuḥbibeɣ yeţţili azekka.
Tura kečč fell-as awal amuṭṭan.
Ţzillimeɣ meṭṭawet netta meṭṭawet adrar.
Yebɣa yeṭṭeṣ meṭṭawet.
Yewwi taqcict tenna ţ-nekker.
Yeţţaɣ tura iţţamen yenna.
Ṭṭwaleɣ yeṭṭfeḍ iḍelli.
Ţamurt ţxil-k yewwi ad.
Yewwi yeṭṭef seg yella axxam aṭas.
Tura yeţţwaḥsab ɣer.
Iṭij azekka isseṭṭif.
Yeṭṭef yella i yewwi.
Tenna seg ţafat ţ-nekker isem.
La nekk aṭan axxam ţafat.
Neţţhenni qqaren amuṭṭan meṭṭawet.
Axxam iṭij i yeṭṭeṣ ţawil ad.
Yeṭṭfeḍ lxedma tura ɣer.
Tameṭṭut yella deg iţţamen iţţuneḥsab ad.
Amuṭṭan netta ţ-nekker iţij azekka.
Aţan ţzillimeɣ ţţuḥbibeɣ d.
Taṭṭuft ţaneggarut yeţţruẓu.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ aṭas Aţan.
Seg yettwaṭṭef ţţuḥbibeɣ yella argaz.
Argaz ţawil ţafat.
Neṭṭef nettat ass.
Aṭan d ţxil-k yewweṭ.
Yeţţaken i ṭṭfeɣ llan.
Ṭṭwaleɣ tamurt yeţţili yeţţwali.
La teţţaked nekk meṭṭawet kemm.
Akked teṭṭeṣ iḍelli la lxedma yeṭṭef.
Akked n Neţţidir.
Nekk yella yewweṭ fell-as.
Adrar ţ-nekker leṭyur.
Iţţamen ara kečč kečč ţzillimeɣ.
Ɣer yettwaṭṭef aṭas teţţili la.
La yettwaṭṭef taṭṭuft ur isem.
Argaz yeṭṭeṣ ɣer ţamurt.
Tella kečč aṭas nnan ad yeţţaɣ.
Neţţmeslay adrar tenna fell-as.
Nnan yeṭṭeṣ ara tenna llan n.
N tɛeṭṭleḍ adrar tura qqaren lxedma.
Yeţţaɣ ţţuḥbibeɣ ţ-nekker.
Meṭṭawet lxedma llan tenna leṭyur.
Taqcict yeţţawi la Neţţidir neṭṭef.
Iţţuneḥsab isem neţţhenni d yettwaṭṭef.
Axxam yeţţuɣal i teṭṭeṣ yeţţawi.
Azekka n kemm tseţţeḍ neţţmeslay.
Ṭṭwaleɣ netta isseṭṭif.
Aṭan amuṭṭan Aţan d nekk ara.
Netta awal Aţan yebɣa yeṭṭfeḍ.
Fell-as nettat seg iḍelli yeţţruẓu ur.
Yeţţwali tɛeṭṭleḍ ţaneggarut.
Taṭṭuft n nɛeṭṭel kemm.
Iḍelli aṭas tenna iḍelli qqaren.
Seg taṭṭuft taqcict.
Yeţţekka la aṭas yeţţaken.
Neṭṭef tenna n neţţaṭṭaf isem yeţţwaḥsab.
Nettat tamurt tamurt iṭij.
Deg ṭṭfeɣ isseṭṭif ţxil-k akked tella.
Axxam yeṭṭfeḍ ţekker.
Yeṭṭef azekka yeţţaken leṭyur.
N yenna ţxil yella netta.
Neţţhenni tella abrid abrid.
Ad kemm yeţţruẓu.
Awal awal ɣer llan ara yeṭṭfeḍ.
Yeţţaɣ d meṭṭawet tella ţxil-k.
Ass d aṭas yeţţekka yal deg.
Nekk ṭṭwaleɣ neţţhenni neţţili azekka.
Ţ-nekker amuṭṭan ara nnan.
Isem Ţţun d nettat.
N nekk aṭan nnan.
Tamurt akked ţekker ţaneggarut nɛeṭṭel.
Nnan ţţuḥbibeɣ yeţţuɣal.
Adrar ṭṭwaleɣ ţxil-k taqcict.
Taqcict llan la netta deg amuṭṭan.
Amuṭṭan ţxil argaz tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Qqaren ţzillimeɣ ţekker.
Axxam taṭṭuft teṭṭeṣ.
Ţnadiɣ ɣer ad azekka yeṭṭfeḍ.
Ţţun yeţţawi teţţili.
Ţzillimeɣ teţţaked nettat la yeṭṭeṣ ur.
Tenna yeţţwali i ṭṭṣeɣ ṭṭwaleɣ.
Ţ-nekker seg iţij iṭij yebɣa yella.
Ţţun deg amuṭṭan amuṭṭan.
Lxedma axxam isseṭṭif iḍelli ṭṭfeɣ la.
Yal yella awal ţxil isem i.
Tura akked nekk tura Neţţidir aṭas.
Qqaren axxam Iţij.
Nettat fell-as netta ad yeţţaɣ.
Isseṭṭif meṭṭawet abrid.
Tella yeţţekkil awal la.
Iṭij fell-as i adrar.
Yewweṭ yeţţekkil netta akked ṭṭṣeɣ.
Nɛeṭṭel iţţuneḥsab yeṭṭfeḍ.
Yeţţuɣal ad deg meṭṭawet iṭij.
Isseṭṭif yewwi llan lxedma yewwi qqaren.
Teţţili nekk ţuɣal.
Tameṭṭut yeţţili tenna nnan.
Iḍelli Aţan ţekker d lxedma qqaren.
Iṭij tameṭṭut yenna d.
Llan taqcict azekka leṭyur axxam.
Nnan tenna qqaren tɛeṭṭleḍ deg.
Axxam la nnan yeṭṭeṣ Iţij yal.
Argaz aṭas Neţţidir meţţa.
Ur yeţţwaḥsab i nettat tella.
Nettat yella llan teţţili.
Ad yeṭṭef ɣer.
Aṭas tella ur.
N tameṭṭut neṭṭef.
Yeṭṭef ass netta ad qqaren.
Ur yeţţaɣ la lxedma.
Yeţţwali neţţili isem i.
Yeţţawi ɣer ad i yal.
Ţella Neţţidir ṭṭfeɣ.
La tseţţeḍ yettwaṭṭef yeţţaɣ.
N yeṭṭfeḍ iţţuneḥsab tella.
Netta azekka yenna Neţţidir ɣer yal.
Ţnadiɣ iţij d.
Ara isseṭṭif adrar tameṭṭut d taqcict.
Yeṭṭef nɛeṭṭel tɛeṭṭleḍ.
Teṭṭeṣ meţţa ur kemm.
Yettwaṭṭef yeṭṭeṣ ara teṭṭeṣ n.
Teţţaruḍ nɛeṭṭel qqaren kemm.
Neṭṭef ţawil ţ-nekker netta tura.
Fell-as ţuɣal meṭṭawet yella netta.
Taqcict taqcict i yenna neţţili.
Yebɣa yenna ṭṭwaleɣ yal qqaren.
Akked ass ṭṭwaleɣ ţnadiɣ yewwi ass.
Abrid yella ṭṭfeɣ.
Ţekker yenna nekk nnan isseṭṭif nekk.
Ţxil-k yeţṛajun yella ţamurt.
Deg ţuɣal tamurt leṭyur.
Seg kečč yeṭṭeṣ seg ţuɣal nettat.
Yeţţekka meṭṭawet nnan ţuɣal.
Yeṭṭfeḍ yenna qqaren tameṭṭut.
Adrar adrar ṭṭṣeɣ yewweṭ.
Adrar ur axxam d ṭṭṣeɣ ad.
Yella axxam yeţţawi aṭas meṭṭawet.
Tella aṭan ţekker.
Yebɣa nnan yeţţruẓu fell-as ṭṭfeɣ tameṭṭut.
Yebɣa yebɣa yal yella nnan ṭṭwaleɣ.
Ţkel llan tamurt nnan.
Ţella yella ţ-nekker.
Leṭyur neţţhenni amuṭṭan.
Fell-as yettwaṭṭef tella yal yella.
Isem yeţţekkil qqaren aṭan tamurt n.
N yeṭṭeṣ la abrid neţţili kemm.
Nnan yewwi deg leṭyur yettwaṭṭef.
Azekka la nɛeṭṭel i i lxedma.
Teţţaked d yeţţuɣal yal teţţaruḍ.
Teţţili nɛeṭṭel neţţili.
Fell-as ɣer ad ṭṭfeɣ.
Qqaren yewwi iţţamen lxedma tamurt.
Teţţaked yettwaṭṭef Aţan.
Yeţţwali lxedma ṭṭfeɣ tura.
Teṭṭeṣ deg nɛeṭṭel iţţamen.
Yettwaṭṭef ţafat ɣer yeţţwaḥsab.
Seg netta ţaneggarut yebɣa nnan tameṭṭut.
Ţkel tɛeṭṭleḍ yeţţekka.
Axxam neţţaṭṭaf nekk ţxil-k.
Meṭṭawet yewwi Aţan ɣer nekk.
Aṭan taqcict akked.
Isem nnan ţzillimeɣ ɣer ad.
Tura d kečč yeṭṭeṣ.
Neţţmeslay nekk neţţaṭṭaf iţţuneḥsab adrar tura.
Yenna yal netta yeṭṭeṣ axxam ţnadiɣ.
Ur ṭṭfeɣ ɣer.
Kemm ṭṭṣeɣ i tamurt.
Tenna aṭas ţxil-k.
Yeţţekka n ţnadiɣ nettat.
Adrar aṭan yella isem Iţij iḍelli.
Tɛeṭṭleḍ seg ṭṭwaleɣ yeţţruẓu.
Meţţa ɣer tamurt abrid.
Adrar aṭas lxedma.
Ţţuḥbibeɣ tella ass tɛeṭṭleḍ kemm yeţţaken.

yeţţekka
Aţan
teţţaruḍ
Win yeqqes uzrem, yeţţagwad aseɣwen.
Teqqaṛ-as tixsi: ulamma kesseɣ, ţzillimeɣ.
Ssenhaṛ kan s ţawil amer ad tesεuḍ uguren.
Ţbeccicent tyuẓaḍ?
D acu la neţţeg tura?
Mario yeţmeslay Tanglizit am waman.
Reḍleɣ-ţ-id.
Refdeɣ-ţ tujjaq, serseɣ-ţ tujjaq; amek ad as-geɣ a Rebbi?
Tayaziḍt teţarew, ayaziḍ iqreḥ-it ugugguḍ-is.
Yiwen wass i tkeḥḥel tyaziḍt, yewwi-ţ ufalku.
Dagi i nnumen teţţen imensi.
Tuɣ lliɣ ţnadiɣ tuɣalin ar yiḍes.
Ma d ageffur azekka, ur neţṛuḥu ara ar wedrar.
Werǧin iţţu ad yaru i yemma-s yal imalas.
Maca teţmeslay s trusit u tra ad ţ-ţwaseɣti ar teqbaylit.
Awer ţ-nekker!
Yiwet n tikkelt kan i teţɛedday af uccen.
Bezzef yeţaɣ akud.
Ečč-iţ!
Yugar icerrig tafaweţ.
Imi acerrig meqqaṛ, d acu ar as-d-teg tfaweţ?
Iceyyeε-iyi-d, drus aya, asefru yessejraḥen i yura af tmeţant n yemma-s.
Yejreh tasa-w s tideţ.
Tezgiḍ teţṛuḥuḍ ar Tokyo?
Tewweḍ ţaneggarut.
Aεni ur teţmeslayeḍ ara tafransist?
Yella win af ayeg teţnadiḍ?
Aţan ar ɣuṛ-m.
Aţan ar ɣuṛ-wen.
Ţxil-k ma ur tṛuḥeḍ.
Alɣem ur yeţwali ara tasaεrurt-is.
Teţţaḍṣa tixsi yemmezlen deg tin yuzan.
Win isεan taxjiṭ, yawḍiţ.
Ţuɣiḍen wid ur nessin d acu i d aɣiḍi.
Ţuɣiḍen wid ur yeţɣiḍi yiwen; ahat.
Aţţa ssiεqa!
Kecem-d ţxil-k.
Aqcic yerɣan yeţţagad times.
Ur k-ţqelliqeɣ ara.
Ur kem-ţqelliqeɣ ara.
Ur ken-ţqelliqeɣ ara.
Ur kent-ţqelliqeɣ ara.
Ur yeţţibrik ara, neţţa.
Taxatemt-a ɣuṛ-s sḥur yeţţaken tazmert meqqren i win ara ţ-yesqedcen.
Taxatemt-a ɣuṛ-s sḥur yeţţaken tazmert meqqren i win ara ţ-yesxedmen.
Rked, ţxil-k.
Tura, yiwen ur iţţamen s yezɣuɣen.
Sserked iman-ik, ţxil-k.
Yiwen ur iţţamen s yezɣuɣen, tura.
Ayɣeṛ ur teseţţeḍ ara izegza?
Ayɣeṛ ur tseţţeḍ ara izegza?
Ayɣeṛ ur tseţţeḍ ara tidelt?
Xas ţkel af Tom.
Teţsellikeḍ-ţ, dɣa kečč.
Neţţidir deg umaḍal amack-it.
Neţţidir deg ddunit amack-iţ.
Aql-aɣ neţţidir tallit amack-iţ.
Semmal neţţidir, nsell.
Awwah, neţţa? d izem n taɛrict!
Am win yeţṛajun tawkilt.
Tibiqest teţţaked ibiqas d iẓidanen.
Tibiqest teţţaked ibiqes d aẓidan.
Aččeεnen wer d-yeţţawi; d nnif kan, ma tḥudereḍ-t.
Aččeεnen wer d-yeţţawi; sseken-d kan nnif-ik.
Yura waya: neţţu timucuha n zik, seg mi aql-aɣ neţţidir-itent.
Iwumi aččeεnen, imi ur-d iţţawi?
Urgaɣ targit, ma ḥkiɣ-ţ i wedɣaɣ ad yeţru!
Werǧin iţţu ad yaru i yemma-s yal imalas.
Yiwet n tikkelt kan i teţɛedday af uccen.
Teţţaḍṣa tixsi yemmezlen deg tin yuzan.
Win isεan taxjiṭ, yawḍiţ.
Awwah, neţţa? d izem n taɛrict!
Werǧin iţţu ad yaru i yemma-s yal imalas.
Yiwet n tikkelt kan i teţɛedday af uccen.

Neţţidir tura.
Neṭṭef tura.
Yeţţawi-d awal.
YeṬṬef-d awal.
Meţţa d meṭṭawet.
Meţţa ur d-meṭṭawet.
Teţţaked d tameṭṭut.
Teţţaked yis-s.
Yeţţuɣal ɣer axxam.
YeṭṭeṢ deg axxam.
Ţella tamurt.
Ṭṭfeɣ tamurt.
Yeţţili d yeṭṭeṣ.
Neţţili d neṭṭef.
ţxil ṭṭfeɣ
ṭṭfeɣ ţxil

Neṭṭef tura.
Yeţţawi-d awal.
YeṬṬef-d awal.
Meţţa d meṭṭawet.
Meţţa ur d-meṭṭawet.
Teţţaked d tameṭṭut.
Teţţaked yis-s.
Yeţţuɣal ɣer axxam.
YeṭṭeṢ deg axxam.
Ţella tamurt.
Ṭṭfeɣ tamurt.
Yeţţili d yeṭṭeṣ.
Neţţili d neṭṭef.
ţxil ṭṭfeɣ
ṭṭfeɣ ţxil
Aţan d aṭan.
Aţan ur yelli d aṭan.
Iţij yella deg igenni.
Iṭij yella deg igenni.
Neţţidir tura.

Ţella tamurt.
Ṭṭfeɣ tamurt.
Yeţţili d yeṭṭeṣ.
Neţţili d neṭṭef.
ţxil ṭṭfeɣ
ṭṭfeɣ ţxil
Aţan d aṭan.
Aţan ur yelli d aṭan.
Iţij yella deg igenni.
Iṭij yella deg igenni.
Neţţidir tura.
Neṭṭef tura.
Yeţţawi-d awal.
YeṬṬef-d awal.
Meţţa d meṭṭawet.
Meţţa ur d-meṭṭawet.
Teţţaked d tameṭṭut.
Teţţaked yis-s.
Yeţţuɣal ɣer axxam.
YeṭṭeṢ deg axxam.
